# Portfolio Cross-Repo Rules

**IMPORTANT**: Hub must NEVER write implementation code (Swift files, tests, views, services) to app repos. Implementation belongs exclusively to each app's own Claude Code session, which can build, test, and integrate against the real Xcode project. Hub's role is limited to research, planning, and documentation.

**EXCEPTION**: Hub MAY update `CLAUDE.md` and `Docs/*.md` files in app repos for cross-repo consistency (e.g., adding `@Docs/` references to design documents, updating project metadata, fixing documentation links). This does not extend to source code, tests, or Xcode project files.

## Cross-Repo Plan Handoff

Plans created in hub that target a specific app repo must be copied to the app repo for implementation:
## App Repos

All app repos live at `../[appname]-app/`. **For READING / pulling / auditing**: discover via `ls -d ../*-app/` — do not rely on hardcoded lists. **For WRITING / distributing content (handoffs, asset bundles, rule syncs)**: use the canonical 140-app registry, NOT a filesystem glob — see § "CRITICAL: Distribution scripts MUST source from canonical portfolio registry" below.

### CRITICAL: Distribution scripts MUST source from canonical portfolio registry (R-PORTFOLIO-INVENTORY 2026-06-04)

**For any script that WRITES to multiple app repos (per-app handoff distribution, asset bundle distribution, doc sync, etc.) — the script MUST source the target app list from `spark-anvil-hub/Docs/REGISTRY_ACTIVE_PORTFOLIO_APPS.txt` (the canonical 140-app registry), NOT from a `ls *-app/` filesystem glob.**
### CRITICAL: Pull Before ANY Cross-Repo Read

**`git pull --ff-only` EVERY repo you are about to read, analyze, or modify — BEFORE the first file read or grep.** This is non-negotiable. Another Claude Code session may have pushed changes from within that app's Xcode project. Stale local clones produce wrong conclusions.
### CRITICAL: Per-repo pull-then-audit BEFORE work, ONE AT A TIME (R420 #910; user-direct 2026-06-01)

**For each target repo in a multi-repo workflow, perform pull-then-audit SEQUENTIALLY — not batched — BEFORE any work touches that repo.** Reinforced per user-direct 2026-06-01 R420 ("pull origin for each repo, one at a time before auditing and doing work").
### CRITICAL: Verify Cross-Repo PRs Merged Before Claiming SHIPPED

After every cross-repo PR (spark-anvil-site, app repos, forgekit, forgesync, forgeplay), confirm the merge:
## Cross-Repo Handoff Protocol

App sessions and hub communicate via paired handoff docs in each repo's `docs/` (or `Docs/`) directory. App sessions don't read hub repo state directly — hub and apps coordinate by writing handoff docs into each other's repos.

### When to write a handoff to hub

From an app session, write a handoff doc when the app needs hub to:
### File naming + location

Both directions live in the same repo's `docs/` (or `Docs/`) — whichever the repo conventionally uses.
### Required structure

Every handoff doc starts with these lines:
### Lifecycle

1. **App session** writes `HANDOFF_FROM_APP_<TOPIC>.md` in app repo, commits, pushes
2. **App session** signals hub by mentioning the doc name in their conversation with the user, who can then surface it to the hub session
3. **Hub session** reads the handoff (via `git pull` of the app repo), implements the change in hub, writes `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_<TOPIC>.md` (canonical, 2026-06-11+) in the same app repo's docs/, commits, pushes — pre-2026-06-11 hub responses used `HANDOFF_FROM_LABSMITH_<TOPIC>.md` (preserved as-is)
4. **App session** (next CC session in the app repo) reads the hub response and continues the integration
### Reference example

CuriosityQuest's bidirectional exchange (2026-05-15) is the canonical example:
### Asset request sub-pattern

A common case: app needs custom art / audio / icon / illustration assets that hub's asset pipelines can generate (or that need a new pipeline). Use the handoff mechanism — don't generate assets in the app repo session, and don't request via Slack / out-of-band.
### Asset generation ownership + handoff requirement (2026-05-19 STANDING RULE; reinforced 2026-06-01 R410 #888)

Hub **owns portfolio-wide asset generation — ALL asset classes, no exceptions** (user-direct 2026-06-01 "you own the audio generation. you own all asset generation"). Apps don't run any generative pipeline: not Gemini Nano Banana Pro/Flash, not Lyria 3, not ElevenLabs/Resemble/Play.ht voice gen, not any future asset-gen vendor. Hub runs them per `GUIDE_ILLUSTRATION_PIPELINE.md` + pipeline-specific guides.

#### R-HUB-OWNS-APP-RESOURCES — hub owns the FULL LIFECYCLE of app RESOURCE assets, including deletion (2026-07-27)

**Hub OWNS app repos' non-Swift RESOURCE assets end-to-end — create · distribute · post-process/modify · AND DELETE (`git rm`) — DIRECTLY, without gating any of it (deletion included) behind a per-app handoff. Ownership of "all asset generation" extends to the assets' whole lifecycle, not just their creation.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-27 (*"hub owns app resources. codify this"*). This is the natural completion of the asset-generation-ownership rule above (hub already CREATES + DISTRIBUTES every asset class) — the missing piece was that hub may also RETIRE/DELETE and otherwise manage those same assets in place.

##### R-HUB-CROSS-REPO-WRITE-FRESH-CLONE — a hub WRITE to another repo (app-repo Docs/resource edit, chapter-frontmatter authoring, handoff commit) uses a FRESH throwaway clone off origin, NEVER the shared sibling working clone (2026-08-21)

**When hub WRITES to another repo — an app-repo `Docs/*.md` edit (e.g. chapter-frontmatter mnemonic authoring), a hub-owned resource change, a handoff commit — it MUST work in a FRESH throwaway clone cloned off origin (`git clone --filter=blob:none … /tmp/wt-<slug>`), NEVER commit-and-push from the shared sibling working clone at `../<app>-app/`. The shared clones are the READ path (CLAUDE.md "For READING: `ls -d ../*-app/`"); for WRITES they are unsafe — they may be stale, carry another concurrent session's WIP, or leave a dirty tree, and a push from them can clobber or entangle that state. This is the app-repo analog of `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-SITE-WORKTREE (site) — same "fresh checkout off origin per write" discipline, generalized to every cross-repo write.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-21 (*"clone the repos for the app handoffs"*). A pathspec `git add <file>` + `git pull --ff-only` first mitigates the risk (as the 2026-08-21 F-MNEMONIC cohort push did — verified clean), but the DEFAULT is a fresh clone, not the shared sibling clone. 🔴 **"FRESH" MUST be a FULL clone — NEVER a `--no-checkout` (or otherwise partial/sparse) clone reused for a WRITE, and NEVER reuse a clone you made for a READ/audit to then commit (2026-09-23 incident, R-NO-MASS-DELETION / ADR-287):** a `git clone --no-checkout --filter=blob:none` leaves the working tree holding ONLY the files you explicitly `git checkout`ed, so a subsequent `git commit` there captures a NEAR-EMPTY tree = a repo-gutting mass DELETION. This is exactly how a hub ACK into the sister `foldcommons-hub` (reusing a `--no-checkout` audit clone) deleted 496 files into FC `main` (PR #124; FC recovered PR #125). So: (a) clone FULL (`git clone --filter=blob:none <url> /tmp/wt-<slug>` — blobless is fine, but NO `--no-checkout`); (b) VERIFY the tree before committing (`git ls-files | wc -l` ≈ expected); (c) the `deny_mass_deletion.py` PreToolUse hook (`workflow.md` § R-NO-MASS-DELETION) is the deterministic backstop that blocks the >50-file-delete commit. A separate clone per PURPOSE (a read-audit clone is NEVER the write clone). 🔵 **ACK-in-a-partner-tree (2026-09-23):** appending an ACK to a partner's inbound `HANDOFF_TO_*` doc that lives IN the partner's tree — keep `> Status:` a valid enum (leave `ACTIVE`/set `DONE`); put the ACK on a `> Ack: …` line or a body ✅-block, NEVER `> Status: ACK` (a non-enum `Status` REDS the partner's frontmatter guard — bit FC twice).
### R-HUB-OWNS-WHETSTONE — the hub owns Spark & Whetstone end to end, like spark-anvil-site (2026-10-04)

**Spark & Whetstone (`nathant99/spark-whetstone`, Astro + Cloudflare Workers, the grown-up sibling site) is HUB-OWNED, on the same footing as spark-anvil-site: the hub writes its code, content, tests and CI directly, with PRs based on `staging` (promote `staging`→`main`, then deploy) and runs the same gates (fresh worktree, screenshot-DoD in both schemes, prod-monitor + dependency-allowlist enrollment per R-NEW-SITE-FLEET-GUARD-ENROLLMENT). A Whetstone row in a concept registry is therefore hub build work under Program #203 after a founder GO, never a `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_*` into spark-whetstone; a row you build gets `status:shipped` + `route:` + `evidence:` that wave (R-HUB-OWNED-SITE-LIFECYCLE; `check_hub_owned_site_lifecycle.py`). A merge to Whetstone `staging` is not live: promote `staging`→`main`, deploy, and curl the route; `check_manual_promote_lag.py` flags unpromoted content on any repo in `REGISTRY_MANUAL_PROMOTE_REPOS.txt`. Two limits: its native Swift app is still a ForgeKit/app-session handoff (hub never writes Swift), and Fold Commons is NOT hub-owned (handoffs into foldcommons-hub only).** Founder-direct 2026-10-04; ADR-291 Amd 23.

**R-VENDORED-ENGINE-IDENTICAL (2026-10-05):** a family site that needs an S&A engine COPIES it byte-identical (manifest of sha-256 + source commit), never re-writes it; fix the source, merge, re-vendor (`vendor_anvil_kernel.py`). Guards: the copy's `vendored.test.ts` (local edit = fork) + `check_vendored_engine_sync.py` vs origin/staging (`REGISTRY_VENDORED_ENGINES.txt`). → ADR-298 §S15.

### R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE — an in-place FIX to a hub-owned resource is SOURCE-ONLY until re-distributed to EVERY consuming surface; verify the fix on the CONSUMERS, not just the hub source (2026-07-30)

**When you FIX/EDIT a hub-owned RESOURCE in place at the SOURCE (a question kit in hub `Resources/Questions/`, an illustration, a humor bank, a config), the fix is NOT shipped until it is RE-DISTRIBUTED to EVERY consuming surface in the SAME wave — the ~app repos (`Resources/Questions/<app>/` via `copy_<kind>_to_repos.sh`) AND the web (`public/play/<app>/…` via the porter, where mounted). A hub-source fix whose distribution step is skipped is SOURCE-ONLY: the hub `Resources/` reads clean, but the iOS fleet (and any un-re-ported web clone) keeps shipping the STALE, unfixed content indefinitely, with ZERO signal. Verify a resource fix's COMPLETENESS by grepping the CONSUMERS (app repos + web) for the fixed pattern — "0 hits in the hub source" is necessary-but-NOT-sufficient; the fix isn't done until the consumers are clean too.** Founder-direct 2026-07-30; sibling of § R-HUB-OWNS-APP-RESOURCES + § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS ("fixed-at-source ≠ shipped").

**A retirement reaches every copy too** (SPM-target bundles, the generator's script): `retire_drama_takes_from_app_repos.py` + `check_retired_drama_takes.py` (P#210 s6).

#### R-KIT-HUB-SOURCE-MAY-BE-STALE — the hub kit copy can DIVERGE from the shipped app/web kits; verify against the CONSUMER before claiming a defect user-facing (2026-08-12)

**The hub `Resources/Questions/<app>/` copy is NOT guaranteed to match the shipped app-repo + web `public/play/<app>/kits/` kits — surfaces can DIVERGE. A SEMANTIC read of the HUB copy can flag "defects" already correct on the shipped surfaces (hub-source-only) or miss a live-only one. Before claiming SEMANTIC-DONE / a user-facing defect, verify the item against the app repo AND web; a fix that only aligns a stale hub copy is SOURCE-HYGIENE (do — but don't over-claim). Inverse of § R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE; an instance of § R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH.** Codified 2026-08-12 (numberverse: 4 hub-copy defects all already-correct on app+web). Detail: `AUDIT_QUESTION_KIT_QUALITY_numberverse_2026-08-12.md`.
### Asset Consumer Audit (2026-05-20 STANDING RULE — closes CubeSensei ASSET_CONSUMER_AUDIT inbound #22)

**Registered ≠ wired.** When an app session adopts a hub-delivered asset handoff (illustrations / mascots / topics / backdrops / modecards / portraits / accessories / icons), the Definition of Done MUST include:
### R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS — every gen pipeline has a completeness guard; pick the SHAPE by blast-radius × criticality; the gap is never silent (2026-07-19)

**Every asset-generation pipeline (illustrations · mascots · cast portraits · chapter art · book covers · badges · music · SFX · texture atlases · icons · audio drama · question kits · biome tiles) MUST have a completeness guard that AUTO-DETECTS a missing asset from on-disk ground truth and SURFACES the gap — a generated asset can never be silently un-shipped. The guard comes in one of TWO shapes, and choosing the right one is load-bearing:**
**⚠ A pending queue builds regenerate but never COMMIT goes stale: guards read live staging (`check_play_mascot_coverage.py` · `check_site_pending_registry_freshness.py`).** **R-MULTIBEAT-FORM-PIN:** no paid multibeat run until `check_multibeat_form_pin_coverage.py` shows the app pinned, portraits read by the prose (DIM8 lesson 48). **COUNT-CRITICAL:** hide stated counts (pin + count gate, lessons 49/53). **R-CAST-PORTRAIT-SPECIES-FORM:** portrait = its chapter's species (`check_cast_portrait_species.py`).

### R-SPAWN-REUSE-ILLUSTRATION-STYLE — a spawned app REUSES the shared `_DEFAULT_STYLE`; a bespoke per-app style is a FOUNDER-GATED exception (2026-08-22)

**A spawned app's generated illustrations (cast portraits · chapter beats · book covers) REUSE the shared portfolio-standard `_DEFAULT_STYLE` (the chunky-cartoon Toca-Boca/Animal-Crossing register in `scripts/pilot_interleaved_ensemble_chapter.py` `STYLE_REGISTRY`) — NEVER a bespoke per-app style unless the founder gates it for a genuine distinct visual NEED (chess look = gambittales/pawnpals). This is evidence-backed, not just cheap: the default is coherence-aligned (Mayer 23/23, d=0.86 — low-extraneous-detail visuals cut cognitive load, matters MORE for ADHD-first surfaces) AND design-system consistency gives cross-product learnability + recognition + the DN "cast IS the curriculum" fleet look. Register a reuse app explicitly as `"<app>": _DEFAULT_STYLE` (or omit it → same fallback). Guarded by `scripts/check_illustration_style_reuse.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_ILLUSTRATION_STYLE.txt`: every bespoke OVERRIDE MUST carry a `# STYLE-OVERRIDE: <slug> — <founder-gated reason>` line; an un-allowlisted bespoke style FAILS `--ci-mode` (silent-style-creep). Composes § R-SPAWN-COMPLETENESS-DIMENSIONS + the Asset reuse policy. Evidence: `Docs/RESEARCH_ILLUSTRATION_STYLE_REUSE_2026-08-22.md`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-22.
### R-HUB-TRAINED-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE — hub MAY TRAIN its own Maia-analog human-like NN opponent (behavioral cloning on OPEN public human game corpora) as a resource-asset gen pipeline, for H2-TARGET board games with NO published model to adopt; band-calibration MANDATORY, compute FOUNDER-GATED, NEVER trained on our own kids' games (2026-08-27)

**Hub MAY produce its OWN human-like NN game engines (Maia-analogs) — the TRAIN-OUR-OWN sibling of the adopt-published `R-WEB-CLONE-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE` (#87/ADR-199) — because training one is CHEAP (pure supervised behavioral cloning, no self-play/search; small residual CNN; ONE reusable pipeline over OpenSpiel's per-game state/legal-move API or the shipped `<game>.ts` engine; ~$1–5 on a spot GPU). It is asset generation, which hub OWNS end-to-end (`R-HUB-OWNS-APP-RESOURCES` — the trained ONNX net is a RESOURCE asset, NOT Swift source; consumed on `/play` via onnxruntime-web exactly like Maia, iOS via a ForgeKit handoff — hub never writes Swift). Hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (a) FIT-ASSESSED, never blanket — train only where H2-TARGET (deep two-player adversarial) + NO published model to adopt (else #87) + an adequate OPEN human corpus (else the solved/simple tail stays H1 — `R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE`, a deliberate REJECT not a gap); (b) 🔴 BAND-CALIBRATION is the ship gate — WE control training, so WE must preserve tween-band human weakness (rating/skill-bin + move-temperature + imperfect-memory + optional piKL-λ); naive BC "averages away" weakness → a net that plays ABOVE-band is a defect on the superhuman-opponent footing (the learnable human mistakes ARE the pedagogy; handicap honestly, `R-DIR-CAPSTONE-BOARD-GAME` F2); (c) the output is an H2 engine → EVERY `R-WEB-CLONE-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE` invariant binds (on-device/single-thread/opt-in/fail-safe/never-default/boundary-only/`R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS`); (d) CORPUS = openly-licensed public human archives with recorded PROVENANCE, and 🔴 NEVER our own users'/kids' games (COPPA / `R-SITE-FEEDBACK` — no per-child data becomes training data); (e) a training RUN is FOUNDER-GATED compute (cheap, single-flight, FOREGROUND — never backgrounded, `R-GEN-NO-BACKGROUND`); (f) HONEST-YIELD (engagement/matched-difficulty/confidence, never a proven skill-transfer/IQ claim). Reference-first on Reversi/gridforge (WThor corpus); tracked by `scripts/check_trained_humanlike_engine_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_TRAINED_HUMANLIKE_ENGINE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on a board-family clone with no row). A shipped trained net also flips its #87 registry row to H2-shipped. Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS; consumer contract = `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-WEB-CLONE-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE.** Founder-direct 2026-08-27; decision `ADR-200`; program #88. → **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-200_TRAIN_HUMANLIKE_GAME_ENGINE_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_TRAIN_HUMANLIKE_ENGINES_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/PLAN_TRAIN_HUMANLIKE_GAME_ENGINE_PROGRAM_2026-08-27.md`.
### R-HUMOR-SEED-COVERAGE — the 70/app humor seed bank (puns·riddles·absurd·facts) is a GUARDED per-app content class; a bank-less active app is never silently forgotten (2026-07-29)

**The humor seed bank (content pipeline #6 — 70/app: 20 puns + 20 riddles + 15 absurd + 15 fun facts, the `ForgeJokeData` schema) is a FIRST-CLASS per-app authored content class on the same footing as question kits (dim 7), cast portraits, icons, and PDF books — so it carries a STANDING self-checking coverage guard, NOT just a one-time audit. Every ACTIVE portfolio app MUST have EITHER an authored bank (`Docs/CONTENT_HUMOR_SEEDS_<APP>.md`) OR a machine-parseable `# HUMOR-EXCLUDED:`/`# HUMOR-DEFERRED:` line in `Docs/REGISTRY_HUMOR_SEED_COVERAGE.txt`. An active app with NEITHER is a silently-forgotten humor-bank gap.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-29 (*"jokes/puns/riddles kits … are silently forgotten. codify this"*). This is the humor-seed sibling of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS (it IS an instance of that pattern) and a per-app content dimension alongside the § R-SPAWN-COMPLETENESS-DIMENSIONS set. **Authored ≠ distributed (2026-08-25):** a bank is SOURCE-ONLY until it lands in its app repo (`HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_HUMOR_SEEDS.md`, R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE; app session writes the Swift) + web `HumorBreak` (§ R-WEB-CLONE-HUMOR-COVERAGE) — guard `check_humor_bank_ios_distribution.py` + `REGISTRY_HUMOR_BANK_IOS_DISTRIBUTION.txt`.
### R-KIT-DISTRACTOR-LENGTH-CUE — the MCQ length cue is fixed in TWO phases (safe-trim DONE + never-pad distractor ENRICHMENT); NEVER pad, always re-audit E9, worst-first, honest-yield (2026-07-30)

**When a question-kit MCQ's CORRECT answer is conspicuously the LONGEST option (`LENGTH_CUE`, Haladyna test-wiseness cue C7 — a test-wise learner can pick the fullest option and be right without knowing), the fix is a TWO-PHASE discipline, and BOTH phases are HARD-guarded against making the bank worse. A kit whose keys systematically out-length their distractors is a VALIDITY/measurement quality defect (not a correctness bug) — fixable, but ONLY by the disciplined method below; a blind length-balancing pass that PADS distractors is itself a defect (it recreates the filler artifact a prior pass stripped).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-30 (*"fix it and codify … add to work queue and prioritize"*), promoting the `AUDIT_KIT_RUBRIC_CUE_TRIAGE_2026-07-25.md` Finding-2 methodology + `RESEARCH_MCQ_ITEM_WRITING_RUBRIC_2026-07-25.md` into a standing rule. This is the kit-quality sibling of § R-HUMOR-SEED-COVERAGE (humor-bank correctness) and the enrichment half of the V610 kit-quality program.

**SYSTEMIC (aggregate) length-cue is a FLEET-WIDE, self-checking sub-axis (Program #66, founder-direct 2026-08-22 *"create a program to audit this issue for the whole fleet/portfolio"*).** The per-item C7 flag (`flag_kit_rubric_cues.py`, ~90% FP) MISSES the real kit-level cue: a kit where the key is strict-longest in a HIGH FRACTION of items (or whose key MEAN length systematically exceeds the distractor mean) is exploitable by "pick the longest" IN AGGREGATE even when every single margin is imperceptible. Tracked by `scripts/check_kit_length_cue_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_KIT_LENGTH_CUE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; FAIL freq>40% or key/distractor mean-ratio>1.15; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a NEW FAIL-tier kit beyond the committed baseline — no forced fleet remediation). Baseline: **2,182/2,197 kits FAIL** (the `generate_*/port_*` family authored complete-sentence keys vs short-phrase distractors). Fix = AUTHOR comparable distractors (NEVER pad, NEVER truncate a correct definition) → re-audit CLEAN → redistribute hub→app→web (§ R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE); worst-first FREE in-session Opus; honest-yield ORDERING signal. Systemic **cue-freedom** sub-axis of § R-KIT-QUALITY-AXIS. Detail: `Docs/AUDIT_KIT_LENGTH_CUE_SYSTEMIC_2026-08-22.md` · queue `q-kit-length-cue`. **⚠ FILLER-PADDING TRIPWIRE (founder-direct 2026-09-05, Program #100 MemoryForge kit lane):** "AUTHOR comparable distractors, NEVER pad" has a cheap wrong way to satisfy the length METRIC — tack a formulaic FILLER clause / meta-hedge / second run-on sentence onto a short distractor ("…so it feels convincing even though it is wrong", "…which is a common mix-up worth avoiding", "…a tempting but mismatched choice") so it measures comparable-length while reading as an obvious reader-detectable TELL. The length-cue guard above is BLIND to this (they pass the mean-ratio). The FIX is to state the misconception COMPLETELY as ONE natural plausible-wrong sentence, never a tacked-on clause (an up-front "make distractors comparable length" instruction to an authoring subagent produced 110 filler-padded MemoryForge distractors that passed length-cue → rewritten before ship). Deterministic tripwire: `scripts/check_kit_distractor_filler.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_KIT_DISTRACTOR_FILLER.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; baseline 0; `--ci-mode` fails on ANY new filler-padded distractor beyond baseline). The THIRD length-cue lens (per-item C7 · systemic mean-ratio · filler-padding).
### R-KIT-QUALITY-AXIS — kit/question QUALITY (item-writing VALIDITY) is a STANDING self-checking axis, DISTINCT from the (complete) answer-key CORRECTNESS marathon (2026-08-12)

**Question-kit QUALITY (is the ITEM well-written? — distractor plausibility · cue-freedom · Bloom spread · reading-load · band/bias fairness, the Haladyna rubric) is a FIRST-CLASS standing self-checking axis — the kit sibling of `workflow.md` § R-FREE-PROSE-QUALITY-DRAIN, DISTINCT from answer-key CORRECTNESS (the completed marathon, `REGISTRY_KIT_QUALITY_COVERAGE.txt`). Tracked by `scripts/score_kit_quality.py` → `Docs/REGISTRY_KIT_QUALITY_SCORE.txt` (deterministic scorecard, SHAPE-B WARN); drained WORST-FIRST on FREE in-session Opus, NEVER-PAD (§ R-KIT-DISTRACTOR-LENGTH-CUE = the cue-freedom sub-axis), redistributed hub+app+web (§ R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE). Score = worst-first ORDERING signal, never a defect count/efficacy claim (flags over-count; DEGENERACY is the reliable one).** → Full detail: `Docs/ADR-112_KIT_QUESTION_QUALITY_AXIS.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_KIT_QUALITY_AXIS_2026-08-12.md`.

**R-KIT-TAIL-TOPIC (P#227):** the generated tail kits (13–16) carry their OWN topic, never kit 1's (the parent report groups by topic). Guard `check_kit_tail_topic_copy.py`; fix with `relabel_kit_topic.py`, every surface.
### R-CONTENT-AUTHORITY-PROCESS — the portfolio MEASURES + MAINTAINS + DISCLOSES its learning-content authority (provenance tier · sampled-review error budget · privacy-safe "Report this item" · misconception registry) — and a content-authority claim is VERIFIED against the item CONTENT before any retire/rewrite, NEVER a filename/catalog pattern (E1 · Program #197 · 2026-09-21)

**The net-new trust/content-authority PROCESS of Program #197's E1 move (ADR-283): the portfolio MEASURES content authority (a per-class PROVENANCE tier "how sure are we?" [engine-verified > corpus-grounded > reviewed > authored > generated-unvetted] + a sampled-review ERROR BUDGET published on `/commons`), MAINTAINS it (a worst-first sampled-review queue drained on FREE in-session-Opus, fix = REWRITE-or-RELABEL never pad/trim/blanket-delete — R-KIT-DISTRACTOR-LENGTH-CUE, redistribute hub→app→web — R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE), and DISCLOSES it (a "Report this item" affordance carrying an `app/kit/item-id` triple ONLY — no child data/PII, mailto/QR, no 3P SDK — COPPA/R-SITE-FEEDBACK; + a portfolio-wide MISCONCEPTION REGISTRY catalog/schema generalizing the LogicQuest fallacy-cast over the fleet-wide `kit_15` corpus that kit distractors + tutor remediation reference). THE LOAD-BEARING INVARIANT: a content-authority claim (esp. "this content is boilerplate / retire it") is VERIFIED against the item CONTENT via a deterministic scan BEFORE any retire/rewrite — NEVER a FILENAME or catalog-name pattern (the ADR-283 §5b catch: the "414-file boilerplate tail" was a filename pattern; the content scan found 0 thin banks across 13,800 items → a blanket retire would have DELETED genuine content, R-KIT-HUB-SOURCE-MAY-BE-STALE / R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT). HONEST-YIELD: authority is a trust/craft/transparency SIGNAL, never a proven learning-gain; on-device — it measures CONTENT, never a learner. COMPOSES (never re-litigates) #66 R-KIT-QUALITY-AXIS · #136 R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH · #190 R-SPARKANVIL-METHOD-CONCEPT-PROGRAM · R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR (remediation consumes the registry) · ADR-177 (vet-before-display). Reference measurement (Wave-1 SHIPPED): `scripts/check_kit_tail_content_authority.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_KIT_TAIL_CONTENT_AUTHORITY.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` reds only on a THIN content REGRESSION). The PROVENANCE-TIER MAP DATA SSOT (Wave-2b SHIPPED, hub base=main): `Docs/REGISTRY_CONTENT_PROVENANCE_TIER.txt` + `scripts/check_content_provenance_tier.py` (SHAPE-B; the deterministic honest-yield guard — an `engine-verified`/`corpus-grounded` over-claim with no real engine/corpus, or a shipped `generated-unvetted` tier, fails `--ci-mode`). The ERROR-BUDGET DATA SSOT (Wave-3 SHIPPED, hub base=main): `Docs/REGISTRY_CONTENT_ERROR_BUDGET.txt` + `scripts/measure_content_error_budget.py` (SHAPE-B honest-yield guard) — an EXHAUSTIVE zero-FP structural scan (excludes the ~90%-FP length_cue/struct/reading-load so the disclosed figure is HONEST) = **0 confirmed defects / 52,925 items = 0.000%** + a reproducible sampled semantic read (cycle-1: 22 items, 0 flagged); `--ci-mode` reds ONLY on a regression above baseline 0. 🔴 The dup/`==key` test is CASE-SENSITIVE — a published budget must never FALSE-ACCUSE correct science-notation content (`Bb`≠`bb`; verify-before-accuse, R-KIT-HUB-SOURCE-MAY-BE-STALE; detail → ADR-283 § Wave-3). The MISCONCEPTION-REGISTRY SSOT (Wave-4 EXTRACTED, hub base=main): `Docs/REGISTRY_MISCONCEPTION_CATALOG.txt` + `scripts/build_misconception_registry.py` (SHAPE-B; indexes the 115 active `kit_15` misconception banks / 8,625 distractor-misconceptions into 11 named fleet-wide FAMILIES [the LogicQuest fallacy-cast generalised] + a per-bank coverage backbone + fleet de-dup; per-item schema rows stay regenerable [COMPOSES, never rebuilds]; the per-distractor family-TAG is the ongoing #66/#136 drain; `--ci-mode` reds only on a coverage regression — a bank dropping out). The TITLE-SPECIFICITY guard (Wave-6): `scripts/check_kit_title_specificity.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_KIT_TITLE_SPECIFICITY.txt` (SHAPE-B) — §5b REFUTES the "491 cosmetic title:=topic relabel" (the 2nd blanket-metadata-transform false-positive: the title/topic mismatches are a legit specific-title/broad-topic structure; a blanket relabel would DEGRADE ~265 specific titles → broad slugs); `--ci-mode` reds on a collapse-to-broad regression (fail-closed-verified). The SITE feature is ✅ COMPLETE — all 3 sub-features on staging: provenance stamp UI [#4068] · Report-item affordance [#4063] · `/commons` error-budget surface [#4079] (TIER-1, base=staging, founder VISUAL gate at the train).** Founder GO 2026-09-21 (Program #197 E1); instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/GUIDE_CONTENT_AUTHORITY_PROCESS.md` + `Docs/ADR-283_PORTFOLIO_AUDIT_ENRICHMENT_MOVE_MINING.md` § Correction.


## An app that says "citizen science" teaches a REAL protocol — practise → record on-device → a grown-up submits; we never submit or collect (R-CITIZEN-SCIENCE-REAL-PROTOCOL; 2026-10-03)

**Any app or `/play` clone that claims "citizen science" (tagline · subject · landing · `apps.generated`) must teach ≥1 real protocol from a named program (NASA GLOBE, the Great Backyard Bird Count / eBird, CoCoRaHS, …), keep observations on the device, give the family an export (CSV and/or a printable sheet), and say honestly how a GROWN-UP submits — or carry a `# CS-EXCLUDED:` line. We never submit, collect, geolocate or require an account; a protocol module makes no network call. Practice items are exact by construction so the engine can grade them. Honest yield: a method and easier real participation, never an outcome claim. Guard `scripts/check_citizen_science_protocols.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_CITIZEN_SCIENCE_PROTOCOLS.txt` (auto-discovers claims on site origin/staging; `--ci-mode` fails on an untracked claim, a missing shipped route, a network call, or a missing grown-ups panel/export). Reference: TerraWatch (site #4403).** Founder-direct 2026-10-03; `ADR-311`; program #217.

## R-SPAWN-COMPLETENESS-DIMENSIONS — a spawned app has ELEVEN silently-forgotten completeness dimensions; each has a standing guard OR a documented exclusion, so none goes dark (2026-07-21; expanded 6→11 2026-07-22)

**A newly-spawned portfolio app is not "done" when its repo + kits land — it opens ELEVEN distinct completeness dimensions (the founding six cast/clone dims + five added 2026-07-22: kits · chapter-gen · DN-S integration · iOS-bundle cast · guides), each of which can be silently forgotten because the *repo builds + looks fine* while the dimension is dark. Every active portfolio app MUST satisfy each dimension OR carry a machine-parseable documented exclusion, and each dimension has an owner + a standing GUARD (a self-checking registry/audit that flags a gap, never a hand-maintained list). An active app dark on any dimension with no exclusion is a coverage defect on the same footing as a missing gen-asset guard.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-21 (*"another dimension of silently forgotten gen is the cast characters for newly spawned app concepts … audit now … codify this lesson"* + *"do we have the canonical registry [of] all of the portfolio characters? codify this"* + *"[web clones of iOS apps] silently forgotten … codify"* + *"i meant cast roster too"*), after the `AUDIT_SPAWN_CAST_WEBCLONE_COMPLETENESS_2026-07-21.md` audit. This is the SPAWN-level umbrella that ties the per-dimension rules into one checklist — the sibling of `R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS` (gen assets) at the whole-app grain.

**⚠ A /play clone with no `dnCast` needs a reason (2026-10-02):** the active-app cast guard can't see web-first clones, so every clone carries a cast or a `# CAST-NONE`/`# CAST-PENDING` line in `REGISTRY_PLAY_CLONE_CAST_COVERAGE.txt` (`check_play_clone_cast_coverage.py --ci-mode`).

**⚠ Registry-add ⇒ web-clone-coverage entry in the SAME wave (2026-08-24, P73 W2).** A spawn that adds an app to `REGISTRY_ACTIVE_PORTFOLIO_APPS.txt` MUST add its `check_web_clone_coverage.py` entry (a clone row OR `# DEFERRED:`/`# EXCLUDED:`) in the same wave — a scaffold-only spawn is a `# DEFERRED: <app> — clone-ready when engine+kits land`; else the guard flags it *silently-forgotten*. Run the guard at spawn close-out.

**⚠ A spawned `/play` clone ships L2-i18n-EXTERNALIZED, not L2-DARK (WCLPM spawn-completeness, 2026-08-26).** A new `/play` clone landing hardcodes its hero/headings/CTAs by default → L2-DARK in every language, silently regressing the completed WCLPM matrix (`localization.md` § R-WEBCLONE-I18N-PARITY-MATRIX). So a spawned clone MUST ship its landing externalized (`data-i18n` hooks + `play.<clone>.*` dict keys across `vi·es·fr·pt·uk·hi`) OR carry a documented exclusion; the auto-discovering guard `scripts/check_webclone_i18n_parity.py` (glob ground truth) is run at spawn close-out so a spawned-DARK clone is never silently forgotten. Sibling of the § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT "ALL new clones MUST be immersed" spawn-completeness backfill (OVERVIEW #18). Fleet 192→200 backfilled 2026-08-26 (site #2805) → 200/200, 0 DARK.
## Visual aids (manipulatives · game boards · diagrams · models) are the PROMINENT + ENGAGING hero of every surface — fleet-wide, apps AND web clones (R-VISUAL-AID-PROMINENCE-ENGAGEMENT; 2026-08-12)

**Every app + `/play` clone's VISUAL AIDS MUST be the surface's PROMINENT (hero `.ff-stage` card, biggest-possible, full recognizable board) + ENGAGING (invites+responds ON the aid; ≥44px affordance; age-band vibrancy — evidence-mandated: on-screen aids hold LESS attention than tactile unless designed to) hero, on a CLEAN/QUIET stage (engagement on the object, not ambient motion — `R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`), across BOTH surfaces (iOS via a `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_VISUAL_AID_PROMINENCE` ForgeUI spec — hub never writes Swift; web fixed directly) + BOTH schemes. Composes above the per-surface prominence rules + the #26 visual-aid-NEEDS program (existence) — THIS is the fleet PROMINENCE+ENGAGEMENT lane. Verdict = the MAIN-SERIAL screenshot-DoD; tracked `check_visual_aid_prominence_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_VISUAL_AID_PROMINENCE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-12; decision `ADR-115`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_VISUAL_AID_PROMINENCE_ENGAGEMENT_PROGRAM_2026-08-12.md`. OVERVIEW program #34.
## Innovative educational games per age band are a standing CROSS-CHANNEL candidate pipeline that FEEDS (never bypasses) R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT (R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM; 2026-08-12)

**Truly-innovative game concepts are mined continuously across ALL channels (web · iOS · Android · physical/tabletop · academic — `R-WEB-CLONE-BACKPORT-MINING`), gap-mapped per band (3-5/6-8/9-14/15-18), tracked `proposed→assessed→approved/rejected→spawned` (`REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt` + `check_innovative_edgame_candidates.py`, SHAPE-B). Bar for "innovative": learning-IS-the-mechanic (DN swap-test — not "a worksheet with a budget") · cross-channel-sourced · band-calibrated · differentiated · depth-honest (app vs MODE vs bundle). It FEEDS `R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT` (re-verify each vs the LIVE `ageBand` census + prior spawn ADRs — `R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH`, the 15-18 "3→14" trap); the founder gates every title (no ceiling bypass); 60-day refresh + completeness-critic. Richest room = 9-14.** Founder-direct 2026-08-12; decision `ADR-116`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_INNOVATIVE_EDGAMES_PER_BAND_PROGRAM_2026-08-12.md` (+ `RESEARCH_INNOVATIVE_EDGAMES_CROSS_CHANNEL_PER_BAND_2026-08-12` + `AUDIT_PORTFOLIO_GAME_MECHANIC_COVERAGE_PER_BAND_2026-08-12`). OVERVIEW program #35.

## Tween loop-native innovative/creative/reflective games are a FOCUSED SPAWN program — borrow the loops tweens ACTUALLY play, make the LEARNING the power system; census-verdict every concept, spawn only the genuinely-uncovered curricula (R-TWEEN-LOOP-NATIVE-GAME-PROGRAM; 2026-08-24)

**The tween (9-14) "Designed to Be Played" spawn program (Program #73) mines the loops tweens ACTUALLY play (Roblox/mobile/TCG megahit mechanics — idle-growth, steal/defend jeopardy, collect→trade→flex, co-presence, creation-as-status, short-session-mastery, physical-digital) and builds games where the LEARNING IS the power system (the field report's design bar P1-P8; P1 is the VETO — a concept a player can prosper in while IGNORING the content is REJECTED). It is DISTINCT from § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (ADR-116, the all-band continuously-refreshed cross-channel PIPELINE): #73 is TWEEN-focused, field-report-driven, and SPAWN-oriented — and it FEEDS ADR-116's registry + R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT (never bypasses the app-count ceiling). EVERY source concept is census-verdicted against the LIVE `apps.generated.ts` (R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) → green(app)/yellow(mode/feature/shell)/red(dup); only genuinely-UNCOVERED curricula spawn (an already-covered domain becomes a MODE, never a duplicate app); each green → a `CONCEPT_SPAWN` doc → founder-gate → spawn. Borrow LOOPS, never assets/names (R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS); meme-AGNOSTIC swappable skins (P5); on-device AI for productive-struggle only (P7, R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR); no engagement-farm economy (P4, R-SITE-FEEDBACK/anti-Gimkit). First wave (Fable field report, 12 concepts): 3 green (signalforge/morphquest/eraforge) · 7 yellow · 2 red. Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_tween_innovative_games_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_TWEEN_INNOVATIVE_GAMES.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN — every concept assessed; every green app-candidate reaches a CONCEPT_SPAWN doc or is founder-rejected).** Founder-direct 2026-08-24; decision `ADR-187`. Sibling of § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM + § R-YOUNGER-BOARD-GAME-FIT (both feed § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT); instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS (self-checking registry).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_TWEEN_LOOP_NATIVE_GAMES_PROGRAM_2026-08-24.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_AND_AUDIT_TWEEN_LOOP_NATIVE_GAMES_2026-08-24.md` + `ADR-187`. OVERVIEW program #73.
## The fleet is content-rich but occasion-POOR — an EVENTS/OCCASIONS layer + the mind-sports ALL-ROUNDER + the two missing mind-sport disciplines (memory, mental-calculation) + AI-proof-by-format teen assessment is a census-verdicted concept program; most "events" are FREE FORMATS of ONE shared Events engine, not new apps; WEB-FIRST; every green DEFERRED+FLAGGED for a per-title founder gate (R-EVENTS-MINDSPORTS-OCCASIONS-PROGRAM; 2026-08-30)

**The 212-app portfolio is content-rich (thousands of kits, ~1,684 cast stories) but OCCASION-POOR — almost nothing turns the content into EVENTS (a club night, a bee, a bracket, a league season, a science fair, a puzzle hunt, a mind decathlon); families/classrooms gather around OCCASIONS, not kits. Program #100 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program for the OCCASION / meta-competition axis — DISTINCT from § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (ADR-116, #35, the all-band megahit-mechanic PIPELINE) and § R-TWEEN-LOOP-NATIVE-GAME-PROGRAM (#73, tween LOOPS) — covering: (a) a shared self-hosted EVENTS engine (Swiss/round-robin/brackets/arena/TGT+bumping/timed-average/bee/buzzer/all-rounder-score/tiebreaks/HMAC receipts) that makes every shipped engine+kit event-ready; (b) the mind-sports ALL-ROUNDER league (the MSO Pentamind DESIGN, kid-simplified — its published formula is a JSON test-vector oracle; NEVER the "Pentamind" MARK); (c) the two classic mind-sport disciplines the fleet lacks — MEMORY sports (loci/PAO) + MENTAL CALCULATION (soroban/Vedic); (d) AI-PROOF-BY-FORMAT teen assessment (oral defence · process-provenance essay · live play · hosting) — the timeliest 15-18 differentiator (the design law "the mentor cannot hand over the answer" AS product). THE LOAD-BEARING FRAME: most "events" concepts (SquadForge-TGT · DecaQuest · BeeForge · BuzzForge-reader · ArbiterForge · the all-rounder league) are FORMATS/PRESETS/LABS over the ONE Events engine — the real standalone-app ask is ~10, not ~35 (a single-mechanic events format → MODE, never a duplicate app; § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT). Nine NON-WAIVABLE invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) ON-DEVICE / no-server / no-PII (host device IS the server); (2) receipts identify BOARDS never children (per-event HMAC keys, expiring — COPPA); (3) NO embarrassing leaderboard — TGT is the DEFAULT competition (group-based, everyone-can-win scoring, ability-matched tables + bumping, public team standings, private individual rank, improvement points; a fixed leaderboard / currency / scarcity / streak-guilt = anti-Gimkit DEFECT, § R-SITE-FEEDBACK); (4) RIDES the V261 transport ladder (Tier 0 pass-and-play → Tier 1 QR/receipts → Tier 2 SparkRoom relay → Tier 3 relay-in-a-box), NEVER a new transport (§ R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES / R-OFFLINE-NEARBY-MULTIPLAYER); (5) AI answer-WITHHOLDING + never a companion (deterministic engines do pairing/scoring/rules; the on-device model only selects Socratic prompts / rewords / summarises the student's own outline — never scoring/arithmetic/facts/free-text-to-a-model-for-a-kid; § R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY / R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR); (6) WEB-FIRST launch, NEW iOS app never an update (the browser clones are OUTSIDE the SB 2420 app-store regime — COPPA/design-codes still bind, already satisfied; adding multiplayer to an EXISTING iOS app trips the Significant-Change re-consent API; web also wins via same-origin cross-app aggregation); (7) ORIGINAL naming (no MSO/competitor marks or trademarked dictionaries; every final name clears § R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS at spawn); (8) HONEST-YIELD — engagement/breadth/occasion/skill/provenance, NEVER a proven learning-gain, never far-transfer-to-IQ (memory/mental-calc = a real SKILL not brain-training), never AI-detection (a process receipt shows PROCESS not PROOF), never certification (external review + year-versioned "illustrative" labels for medical/first-aid/tax/legal); (9) FEEDS § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116 — every green is DEFERRED + FLAGGED for a per-title founder gate (a blanket "everything approved" authorizes the PROGRAM, not 35 auto-spawns; § `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT / no ceiling bypass). Census (LIVE origin/staging, 2026-08-30): 35 concepts → 10 green · 22 yellow · 3 red (OrbitForge≈starforge · LinguaQuest-HS≈linguaquest · TinkerQuest≈machineforge). Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_events_mindsports_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_CONCEPTS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN — every row well-formed; every green app-verdict tracked to a gate).** Founder-direct 2026-08-30 (analyze the 3 attached briefs / audit the fleet / deep-web-research+think-harder / create a NEW program to track). Decision `ADR-216`; research `RESEARCH_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_OCCASIONS_2026-08-30.md`; plan `PLAN_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_OCCASIONS_PROGRAM_2026-08-30.md`. Sibling of § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM + § R-TWEEN-LOOP-NATIVE-GAME-PROGRAM (all feed § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT); instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS (self-checking registry).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-216_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_OCCASIONS_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_OCCASIONS_2026-08-30.md` + `Docs/PLAN_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_OCCASIONS_PROGRAM_2026-08-30.md`. OVERVIEW program #100.

## The AlphaFold-era structural-biology story + the sibling Fold Commons portfolio are mined for S&A reasoning concepts via a census-verdicted program — the flagship "confidence" move is ALREADY the shipped #93 mechanic (compose, never re-propose); migrate NO Fold Commons repos (category mismatch) but reroute 2 blocked concepts + bridge assets; honest-yield binds (confidence = awareness, never a proven gain) (R-ALPHAFOLD-ERA-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-08-30)

**Program #101 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the AlphaFold-era story (open-science→enclosed-asset · confidence≠correctness · ~90% trial attrition · the commons that built it) + the SEPARATE sibling Fold Commons native structural-biology portfolio (`/Volumes/Data/Projects/alphafold-portfolio/`, Reference category) for Spark & Anvil reasoning app/mode concepts — DISTINCT from § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (ADR-116, #35) / § R-TWEEN-LOOP-NATIVE-GAME-PROGRAM (#73) / § R-EVENTS-MINDSPORTS-OCCASIONS-PROGRAM (#100), and it FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. Load-bearing invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE — the briefs' flagship "calibrated confidence / confidence dial / SureForge" is ALREADY the SHIPPED Program #93 `calibrated-confidence` mechanic (ADR-208, Confidence Ledger ×4 clones); recorded COVERED-BY-MECHANIC, never re-proposed — compose ABOVE #93 (the `two-meters`/`truth-panel`/`jagged-assistant` mechanics likewise already ship); the two distinct deltas (fleet-wide answer-submission DIAL / a dedicated calibration APP) feed #93/ADR-116, and the rec is WIDEN #93, do not scaffold a 10th app (the briefs' own sequencing). (2) HONEST-YIELD (web-verified 2026-08-30) — calibration is trainable but INCONSISTENT and FEEDBACK-ALONE (even proper-scoring-rule feedback) does NOT reliably improve it (Martin 2025); a confidence/calibration surface is a metacognitive-AWARENESS/reflection SIGNAL, NEVER a proven calibration-gain (AnchorQuest register; matches #93's guardrail); any outcome claim is a defect. (3) FOUNDER-GATE PER-TITLE — every green|yellow app row is DEFERRED + FLAGGED (a blanket "everything approved" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the spawns; § `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT / no ceiling bypass) and logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt`; on a per-title GO → CONCEPT_SPAWN → scaffold. (4) FOLD COMMONS MIGRATION — migrate NO repos (a native structural-biology VIEWER is a category mismatch for a browser-GAME fleet), REROUTE 2 blocked concepts (ProteinQuest = game-design-gap · CellTour = cell-renderer-gap — build fresh as S&A games, Fold Commons links in, no code moves), BRIDGE assets (161-report archive as curriculum [budget the readability rewrite] · ResearchAtlas citation-grounding for AI-tutor auditability · CC0/CC-BY structure bundles + Mol*/3Dmol.js for the SDK-gated science flagship); NEVER couple the 208 web clones to FoldKit (Swift-only); NEVER imply the app folded a protein (precomputed structures only). (5) NAMING — never AlphaFold/AF/Open-Fold; FoldQuest/FoldSmith collide with the Fold Commons namespace → ShapeForge/ShapeQuest (§ R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE / R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS). (6) SDK-GATED science flagship — ShapeForge needs a NEW third-party render SDK (Mol* MIT / 3Dmol.js BSD) → a founder-gated § `spark-anvil-website.md` R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE ADR + a curated offline structure bundle; no confidence/commons concept needs a renderer. Census (LIVE 208-app registry, 2026-08-30): 1 covered-by-mechanic · 1 🟢 new gap (CommonForge, the #1 recommended GO) · 4 🟢/🟡 founder-gated candidates (confidence-dial-fleetwide · benchforge · bottleneckforge · shapeforge) · 2 modes · 2 Fold-Commons reroutes. Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_alphafold_era_concepts_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_ALPHAFOLD_ERA_CONCEPTS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-30. Decision `ADR-217`.

## Arthur Benjamin's "mental math as theater" is mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program — the generalizable move is a PERFORMANCE-FIRST LENS across the dense math cluster (unit-of-progress = shows performed, not solo streaks), NOT 8 apps; ONE 🟢 flagship (ShowMath) + 7 🟡 modes; every build founder-gated; honest-yield binds (performance = engagement, never a proven learning-gain) (R-MATHEMAGIC-PERFORMANCE-PROGRAM; 2026-08-30)

**Program #102 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the Arthur Benjamin *Secrets of Mental Math* brief (8 concepts + sparks) — DISTINCT from § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (ADR-116, #35) / § R-TWEEN-LOOP-NATIVE-GAME-PROGRAM (#73) / § R-EVENTS-MINDSPORTS-OCCASIONS-PROGRAM (#100) / § R-ALPHAFOLD-ERA-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#101), and it FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. Load-bearing invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE against the LIVE census (216 apps, origin/staging, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) — the math cluster is DENSE, so most concepts are MODES not apps: *Guesstimate* is LARGELY COVERED by numbersense (estimate-first + per-kid calibration card + daily) and *House Always Wins* by chanceforge (virtual-casino + EV + variance) → a new estimation/probability app = 🔴 duplicate; the genuine whitespace is the PERFORMANCE-first framing, absent portfolio-wide. (2) THE GENERALIZABLE MOVE = a reusable LENS ("performance-as-the-unit-of-progress / reveal-the-secret / checking-is-a-trick / algebra-is-the-backstage-pass"), NOT 8 apps — 1 🟢 standalone flagship (**ShowMath**, the umbrella absorbing any-day/magic-square/missing-digit/beat-the-calculator as routines) + 7 🟡 MODES on existing clones (say-it-first→reckonquest · any-day→daily surface · guesstimate→numbersense · nine-detectives→sleuthlab/vaultforge · cottage-faces→tutorforge · tile-proofs→discretequest · house-always-wins→chanceforge), with **tile-proofs (deepest moat) + nine-detectives the two FOUNDER-ELEVATE-to-app candidates**. (3) HONEST-YIELD (web-verified 2026-08-30) — performance/audience learning is well-evidenced (Kobayashi 2024 protégé meta g≈0.27, g≈0.50 interactive) but a *performance* of a self-working trick is engagement/practice-MOTIVATION, NEVER a proven learning-gain; calibration is teachable-but-transfer-limited; left-to-right is a number-sense STRATEGY (superiority-for-all is rhetoric-heavy); tile-proofs makes proof tactile (thin outcome studies) — any outcome/"closes-the-gap" claim is a defect. (4) FOUNDER-GATE PER-TITLE — a blanket "everything approved" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the ShowMath spawn nor the mode BUILD drain (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT / no 216-app-ceiling bypass); DEFERRED + FLAGGED in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt`; on a per-title GO → CONCEPT_SPAWN → scaffold (or a web-first `/play` clone, R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS, the ceiling-safe path). (5) ANTI-GAMBLING — *House Always Wins* is a fairness-lab (expose-the-edge, no gambling aesthetics, no wagering-for-reward); RE-AFFIRMS ADR-113 (Apple rates even simulated gambling 17+); does NOT reopen it. (6) NO NEW TRANSPORT — the performance volunteer-console + class duels ride the LIVE V261 room transport (ADR-041), never a new one; on-device/no-PII/COPPA. (7) IP HYGIENE — original patter/examples; NO Benjamin name/likeness/"Mathemagics" branding on app names/icons/store marketing (brief Part 6); "inspired by the work of Arthur Benjamin" on an About screen only. Census (LIVE 216-app, 2026-08-30): 1 🟢 (ShowMath, founder-gated to spawn; CONCEPT_SPAWN authored) · 7 🟡 modes/features · 0 🔴 · 0 auto-greenlights. Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_mathemagic_concepts.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_MATHEMAGIC_CONCEPTS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, mirrored into `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`).** Founder-direct 2026-08-30. Decision `ADR-219`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-219_MATHEMAGIC_PERFORMANCE_FIRST_MATH_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_MENTAL_MATH_AS_THEATER_BENJAMIN_2026-08-30.md` + `Docs/PLAN_MATHEMAGIC_PERFORMANCE_FIRST_MATH_PROGRAM_2026-08-30.md` + `Docs/CONCEPT_SPAWN_SHOWMATH_2026-08-30.md`. OVERVIEW program #102.

(2) NET WHITESPACE = the shared **ProjectSpine** library (🟢 LIBRARY, R-LIBRARY-REPO-SPAWN) + **5 clean 🟢 band-gap apps** (PlanPals·HelpPals·CauseQuest·CapstoneForge·ActionQuest) — NOT "13 apps"; 7 🟡 land as MODES/decks/band-extensions. (3) FOUNDER-GATE PER-TITLE — "everything approved" authorizes the PROGRAM, not spawns (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT / no 225-app-ceiling bypass); every 🟢 DEFERRED+`pending-founder` in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt`; on a GO → CONCEPT_SPAWN or a web-first `/play` clone (R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS). (4) AI-AS-APPRENTICE is the answer-WITHHOLDING moat (asks, structurally CANNOT make the artifact — R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY); process-portfolio-to-book + oral defense (VivaQuest) = AI-proof by construction (R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING). Also binds (→ ADR-228): privacy-by-architecture inherited (on-device, no accounts/PII, no minor-facing chat, audio=COPPA PI); 15-18 = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED (text-forward, NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio — CRITICAL) + younger register/layout rules + V261-ladder Together (no new transport); HONEST-YIELD (classroom d≈0.65-0.85 heterogeneous, home-PBL effect UNMEASURED → "real-world projects / AI asks-never-answers / every project becomes a book", outcome language only after a pilot). Census (LIVE 225-app, 2026-09-03): 5 🟢 apps + 1 🟢 LIBRARY · 7 🟡 · 1 elevate · 0 auto-greenlights. HEADLINE FINDINGS: (a) nobody is building the Arena for children — BGA's age-15 floor leaves 3-14 open; (b) 3-8 should be a WAVE not a trickle (8 of the 14 greens are 3-8) with a "Table Companions" BUNDLE option (TurnTots+ScorePals+FairPlayPals as one app/shared shell); (c) the high green-ratio is HONEST — every green sits in a confirmed 0-hit niche BGA's floor forecloses, not padded. ## Word games (Scrabble/Words-With-Friends/the 2026 word-game boom) are mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program (Report No. 76 "The Missing Tile") — the load-bearing find: competitive Scrabble trains LETTERS, not MEANINGS ("semantic deemphasis", no far transfer), so every concept obeys the MEANING RULE (score meaning) — the single design law; census = 12 clean 🟢 gaps (the tile-laying crossword game absent at every band + the clue-game + the word-race + the word Dojo + lexicography + corpus/historical linguistics) + 4 🟡 modes + 3 infra moves (ForgeLex · the tile engine · the Meaning Rule); every 🟢 founder-gated; honest-yield (spelling/orthographic-fluency/vocabulary-with-meaning, never IQ) (R-WORD-GAME-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-04)

concept-approval ≠ registry-add) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) MODE-by-default (single-mechanic → mode/band-ext) + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS (**NEVER "Scrabble"** on a kid surface); ## Music, instrument, karaoke & singing apps are mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program (Report No. 78 "In the Child's Key") — the load-bearing thesis: every music app hears an ADULT, none hears a CHILD as a child; the published design law is the CHILD-KEY RULE (measure the child's range first, never ask for a note outside it — pedagogy + vocal health), and the 2025 COPPA voice-biometric regime makes on-device the moat; §5b GROUND-TRUTH catch — the brief's "12 apps, ZERO microphones" audit is LITERALLY REFUTED (waveforge=voice-oscilloscope) but substantively CONFIRMED (nothing LISTENS to a child SING as a child); census = 13 clean 🟢 gaps + 3 🟡 + 2 infra (ForgePitch · ForgeSongbook); every 🟢 founder-gated; honest-yield (sings-in-tune/keeps-a-beat/reads-notation, never "smarter"/far-transfer) (R-MUSIC-SINGING-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-04)

**Program #115 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the founder-supplied music/instrument/karaoke/singing brief Report No. 78 "In the Child's Key" (16 concepts across 4 bands + 2 shared-infra moves + 10 bench) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the FOURTH sibling of #112 (ADR-229, Report No. 74) + #113 (ADR-230, Report No. 75) + #114 (ADR-231, Report No. 76). It COMPOSES the 12 shipped music/sound apps (`beatforge`/`harmonyforge`/`synaforge`/`soundsphere`/`waveforge`/`melodymice`/`rhymereef`/`lyricforge`/`motiflab`/`clefquest`/`genforge`/`ensemblequest` — none of which LISTENS to a child sing) + rides the existing infra (`ForgeVoice` No.77 family-recorded-voices · `ForgeTable` No.75 same-room transport) and FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. The load-bearing NET-NEW infra is the on-device LISTENING engine `ForgePitch` (no shipped app hears voice/instrument for pitch feedback; waveforge's mic is a physics oscilloscope) + the licensing-clean `ForgeSongbook` — both ForgeKit handoffs (hub never writes Swift), the cheapest-per-face first move all 16 concepts ride. Eleven hard invariants bind (ADR-232; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / dedup-HARD vs the LIVE 234-app fleet (origin/staging, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) — all 28 names free; the brief's "zero microphones" audit corrected (waveforge listens; the honest claim is "first to listen to a child SING as a child"); `MasteryForge`=slug `alcumusforge` (exists), `SteadyRealm` absent (stale ref); the brief's 221/228 counts are drift (234 canonical); (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every 🟢 (+ app-disposition 🟡) DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything approved / prioritize it only" authorizes the PROGRAM, not 13 spawns; concept-approval ≠ registry-add) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) **the CHILD-KEY RULE** — measure the child's comfortable range first, present every song inside it, never ask for a note outside it; re-check as ranges grow, monthly for 11+ (voices change); a pedagogy rule (match pitch inside a fifth D4–A4 first — Rutkowski/Welch) AND a vocal-health rule (never asks a child to strain); the published Method-page law beside No.76's Meaning Rule; (4) **on-device / no-collection COPPA moat** — audio analyzed in memory + discarded; the range profile stored as NOTE NUMBERS (a music setting, not a voiceprint); recordings only where a parent chose, on-device, exported via Files behind a gate (HeritageQuest precedent); NEVER cloud-scored singing for under-13s; the 2025 COPPA amendments (voice=PII, voiceprint=biometric) are the structural CAUSE of the whitespace; (5) **deterministic grading, mascot narrates, NO autotune** — DSP scores; the on-device model talks about the score, never computes it (§ `distributed-narrative.md` R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY); autotune ("flatters") is the anti-pattern; (6) **licensing-clean ForgeSongbook** — public-domain (US ≤1930) + traditional (composition PD, arrangement/translation in-house) + originals (CC BY-NC-SA) + the family's own; synthesized MusicXML backings (no master/sync license, *Leadsinger v. BMG*); per-song provenance; **NO licensed pop**, the honest trade; (7) **voice-change-aware WITHOUT labels** — range→part mapper on lowest/highest notes only, never asks gender, never says "your voice is breaking" ("today your part is cambiata"); (8) **same-room-only ensembles** (BellTots/ChoirQuest-Together/BatonQuest ride ForgeTable topologies 1+2 only; children's singing never SENT anywhere); (9) MODE-by-default for thin single-mechanics + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS (**NEVER "Recorder Karate"/"Boomwhackers"**; BellTots ships its OWN palette); 15-18 = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED text-forward, **NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio** (voicedojo/cadenceforge/mixforge/demoforge); (10) HONEST-YIELD — sings in tune within a known range / keeps a beat / reads notation / holds a part / plays back a riff (musical + measurable); **NEVER "smarter" / far-transfer** (Sala & Gobet 2020 g=0.056 n.s. with active controls; the transfer debate is contested — don't sell it); vocal-health-as-design (a high-intensity-minutes budget for 12+; loudness relative not dB; medical-adjacent language non-diagnostic); (11) ZERO paid gen (census+codify+track+guard; no repos/CONCEPT_SPAWN/Gemini) + site PRs=STAGING / hub codification=main; ONGOING + GENERALIZED. Census (LIVE 234-app, 2026-09-04): 2 infra (forgepitch/forgesongbook) · 13🟢 clean gaps (humtots/drumtots/lullatots/solfapals/recorderpals/choruspals/strumpals/choirquest/standforge/riffquest/voicedojo/cadenceforge/mixforge) · 3🟡 mode/band-ext/thin (belltots/batonquest/demoforge↔lyricforge) · 10 bench (fretforge/keysquest/scoreforge→ReelForge/timbrequest→EraForge-ClefForge/sectionquest/boxforge/roundpals→ChoirQuest/looppals/pulsequest/jamquest) · 0 auto-greenlights. Registered REVIEWED as a #109 instance (`REGISTRY_FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_COVERAGE.txt` via a `# CB-EXCLUDED:` line, the #112/#113/#114/PBL precedent). Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_music_singing_concepts.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_MUSIC_SINGING_CONCEPTS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on a malformed row or an untracked build-worthy concept).** Founder-direct 2026-09-04 (*"add a new axis to the fable-reviewed concept mining program using the research brief Report-78-In-the-Childs-Key … create a new program to track this work if needed. prioritize it only … everything is approved. go with your recs. do not stop"*). Decision `ADR-232`. Sibling of § R-WORD-GAME-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#114) · § R-BOARDGAME-MINDSPORT-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#112) · § R-BOARDGAME-PLATFORM-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#113) · § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (#35) · § R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING (#109, the composing parent); instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS. Composes § `cognitive-accessibility.md` R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY (the InclusionForge haptic-track sibling — a Core Haptics track per ForgeSongbook song).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-232_MUSIC_SINGING_CONCEPTS_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_IN_THE_CHILDS_KEY_MUSIC_SINGING_2026-09-04.md` (source brief) + `Docs/RESEARCH_MUSIC_SINGING_CONCEPT_MINING_2026-09-04.md` (census synthesis). OVERVIEW program #115.

## Museum, camp & visit apps are mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program (Report No. 78 "Thirty-Two Classrooms" — HMNS) — the load-bearing thesis: the VENUE APP is a solved negative (2.47% adoption) → never build "the museum app", build the CHILD'S OWN VISIT (before/during/after, at any museum/zoo/park; institutions = optional offline open-license packs, never accounts/servers); the census surfaces TWO ladders (visit + curation) answering Report 77's "diamond, not a ladder" critique with a named next-app at every band 3→18; §5b CATCH — the brief's "ShelfPals" COLLIDES with Report-75/#113's ShelfPals → RENAMED GalleryPals, GlyphPals overlaps linguaquest's "Glyph" cast char (mechanic distinct); census = 16 clean 🟢 gaps + 1 infra (ForgeVisit) + 9 bench; every 🟢 founder-gated; honest-yield (informal-STEM moves ATTITUDES g≈0.21, never a proven achievement gain — "a week is a spark, the portfolio is the anvil") (R-MUSEUM-VISIT-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-04)

**Program #116 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the founder-supplied museum/camp/visit brief Report No. 78 "Thirty-Two Classrooms" (HMNS; 16 concepts across 4 bands + 1 shared-infra move + 9 bench) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the FIFTH sibling of #112 (ADR-229, Report No. 74) + #113 (ADR-230, Report No. 75) + #114 (ADR-231, Report No. 76) + #115 (ADR-232, music). It FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. The load-bearing NET-NEW infra is `ForgeVisit` — the shared on-device "visit" data model (a PLACE typed-never-geolocated + before-plan + during-checklist + after-page + a photo-set of THINGS-not-people + a five-icon sensory profile) the five visit-ladder apps read/write; a ForgeKit + content handoff (hub never writes Swift), the cheapest-per-face first move. Eight hard invariants + ten design principles bind (ADR-233; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / dedup-HARD vs the LIVE 235-app fleet (origin/staging, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) AND the sibling reports — all names free on live slugs, but the brief's ShelfPals collides with the #113 sibling CONCEPT (dedup must cover sibling-concept names, not just live slugs) → RENAMED GalleryPals; GlyphPals overlaps linguaquest's shipped "Glyph" cast char (mechanic distinct → 🟢, naming look at spawn); chronoquest ships curation-as-REWARD (not a downgrade of the curation-CRAFT concepts); (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every 🟢 DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything approved / prioritize it only / go with your recs" authorizes the PROGRAM, not 17 spawns; concept-approval ≠ registry-add) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) MODE-by-default + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS at spawn; (4) **the ten design principles** — the 2.47%-rule (venue-agnostic, offline open-license packs, no accounts/servers) · role-before-content · the take-home law (every session ends PRINTABLE) · the five-icon sensory grammar (Loud·Dark·Changing-Lights·Tactile·Break-Area) as a first-class data type · before/during/after with "during" deliberately LIGHT · at-fifteen-the-child-is-STAFF · **photograph THINGS not people** (no face detection, nothing off device, SB 2420 + COPPA-2026) · **no AI grading of a child's words** (on-device models play the CONFUSED VISITOR, self-scored rubrics — R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY / R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR) · **open collections only** (CC0/PD, verify before shipping, no HMNS imagery) · **honest-yield** (attitudes g≈0.21, never a proven achievement/IQ gain); (5) 15-18 (exhibitforge/provenancequest/domeforge/docentforge) = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED text-forward, **NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio**; domeforge's fulldome-export + AVP scene are DEVICE-SPECIFIC → a /play clone SKIPS them (R-WEB-CLONE-DEVICE-FEATURE-SKIP), the authoring core ports; (6) on-device / no-PII / COPPA / dark-safe / WCAG / age-band / boundary-only / engine-verified; (7) ZERO paid gen (census+codify+track+guard; no repos/CONCEPT_SPAWN/Gemini); (8) site PRs=STAGING (ADR-154) on any /play kernel + hub codification=main; ONGOING + GENERALIZED (a new HMNS/museum-derived concept auto-discovers into the registry). Census (LIVE 235-app, 2026-09-04): 1 infra (forgevisit) · 16🟢 clean gaps [3-5: visittots/curiotots/bonetots/fluttertots · 6-8: stamppals/gallerypals/glyphpals/sensepals · 9-14: mohsforge(THE cleanest gap — no mineralogy/gemology on the fleet)/fieldforge/backstagequest/dioramaforge · 15-18: exhibitforge/provenancequest/domeforge/docentforge] · 0🟡 · 9 bench (dinopals/rollpals/geodetots/pendulumpals/wattforge/campforge/keeperquest/glamlab/dometots; 2 open crosswalk gaps = 6-8 dinosaur + 6-8 motion) · 4 portfolio-move candidates (five-icon grammar portfolio-wide · 2.47% design law on /method · take-home law · Camp-at-Home crosswalk) · 0 auto-greenlights. ## The THIN bands + the 15-18 rites-of-passage are mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program (Report No. 77 "Forged, Not Yet Found") — the load-bearing outcome is the §5b DEDUP (4 of 16 already tracked/covered: EchoTots+SignQuest→P#109 · MootForge→P#100 · TempoForge→StandForge P#115), so the honest census is 12 clean 🟢 band-gap gaps + 1 infra (ForgeSensors, the "one module three apps" P10) + 4🔵; the distinctive thesis is filling 3-5/6-8/15-18 with the "screen-faces-the-grown-up / Together" formats the 2026 free-nonprofit field lacks; every 🟢 founder-gated; honest-yield (format-fit/engagement, never a proven outcome) (R-BAND-GAP-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-04)

**Program #117 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the founder-supplied band-gap brief Report No. 77 "Forged, Not Yet Found" (16 concepts across 4 bands + 11 portfolio moves) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the FIFTH sibling of #112 (ADR-229, Report No. 74) + #113 (ADR-230, Report No. 75) + #114 (ADR-231, Report No. 76) + #115 (ADR-232, Report No. 78). It COMPOSES the thin-band literacy/EF/music apps + the 15-18 wave + rides the existing infra (`ForgeVoice` No.77 family-recorded-voices) and FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. The load-bearing OUTCOME is the §5b DEDUP — Report 77's 16 concepts are NOT 16 novel mechanics; its real value is a POSITIONING thesis (9-14 holds 70% of playable apps, the two youngest bands ~13%, and the youngest's "designed for face-to-face play" promise is contradicted by every app being single-child tap-the-screen), so the concepts fill the THIN bands (3-5, 6-8) + the 15-18 rites-of-passage with the "screen-faces-the-grown-up / Together / screen-down" format the 2026 free-nonprofit field (Khan Kids/PBS/Duolingo ABC/ScratchJr) does NOT offer. Six hard invariants bind (ADR-234; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / dedup-HARD vs the LIVE 235-app fleet (origin/staging apps.generated.ts @ 8bae6fdd4, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) AND the existing concept programs — Report 77 tripped the #1 FCBM failure mode FOUR times, caught + NEVER re-greened: **EchoTots**→already a P#109 candidate (first-L2-exposure, 3-5) + heritage sub-part covered by **LullaTots** (P#115) · **SignQuest**→already a P#109 candidate (ASL-as-a-language, 9-14; fallback name HandsQuest, SignalForge exists) · **MootForge**→already assessed under **P#100** (mock trial, 15-18) · **TempoForge**→COVERED by **StandForge** (P#115 band/orchestra/choir practice companion) → fold sub-parts, never a duplicate build; a short honest "4 of 16 already covered" is the correct outcome, not a padded 16-green; (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every 🟢 DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything is approved / prioritize it only" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the 12 app spawns; concept-approval ≠ registry-add) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) DESIGN-CONSTRAINT INHERITANCE (already the posture) — on-device only · no free-text chat · AI (where any) a teachable novice/rules-prompter · a small recurring cast whose behaviour IS the rule (R-DN-PARITY) · Together or "screen-faces-the-grown-up" first-class (P9 Grown-up mode) · browser-first + native upgrade (R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS) · **NO camera, NO location** in any concept · microphone only as an on-device analyser, never a recorder-by-default; the **P10 "one module, three apps"** = the infra move **ForgeSensors** (CoreMotion + torch + compass/heading, a ForgeKit handoff — hub never writes Swift) serving WiggleTots/ShadowTots/HorizonQuest; (4) 15-18 greens (RoadForge/BallotQuest/MentorDojo) = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED text-forward CASE-NARRATIVE, **NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio** (CRITICAL spawn gate); (5) PORTFOLIO MOVES P1-P11 are OUT OF SCOPE for a CONCEPT program — only P4 (Little Learners charter → the 3-5 four), P9 (Grown-up mode), P10 (ForgeSensors) feed here; the rest (P1 distribution/SEO, P2 manifest, P3 native/browser framing + SB 2420/age-rating readiness, P5 age-rating audit, P6 ESA/TEKS, P7 candor, P8 fiscal-sponsor) route to the site/discoverability/compliance/ops lanes, recorded so the calls are explicit; (6) HONEST-YIELD (format-fit / engagement / band-appropriateness / a named field gap; the brief's evidence anchors — Whitehurst/Mol dialogic reading, Therrien repeated reading, Cohen-Kulik-Kulik peer tutoring, Walkington personalisation, Holbein-Hillygus pre-registration, WHO 2019 under-5 — are the cited literature, re-checked before any method-page claim, NEVER a proven outcome) + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE (standpals[money] vs standforge[music P#115]) + ZERO paid gen (census+codify+track+guard; no repos/CONCEPT_SPAWN-repos/Gemini) + hub codification=main / site PRs=STAGING · ONGOING + GENERALIZED (a future band-gap brief = a new #117 wave or #109 instance). ## Ludii & general-game-systems are mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program (the "Ludii, decoded" brief) — the load-bearing outcome is the STRONGEST §5b DEDUP of the series: the brief's 18-game roster is ~90% ALREADY SHIPPED (millsong/foxpals/seedsong/pipquest/leaptale/gridforge[Hex+Dots]/stonesong/nimforge/generalstale/…) so the #1 FCBM failure mode is tripped ~9× → recorded BLUE, never re-greened; the genuine net-new is the archaeoludology RECONSTRUCTION mechanic (the brief's OWN flagship) + a Vietnamese heritage-game content wave + a heritage atlas; and — after the founder's "clean-room engine + games? think harder" push + deep research — the "Anvil Ludeme Engine" is UPGRADED from a too-quick reject to a founder-gated 🏗 INFRA/LIBRARY (clean-room-legal, reuse-referenced, justified by the reconstruction+design-forge+content-velocity+oracle capabilities the per-game engines can't provide, shipped 13 NOT rebuilt); every 🟢/infra founder-gated; honest-yield (near-transfer/heritage-literacy, never IQ; the reconstruction mechanic = hypothesis-testing, never "deduces the truth") (R-LUDII-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-04)

**Program #118 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the founder-supplied Ludii brief "Ludii, decoded: research brief and Spark & Anvil app concepts" (`~/Downloads/ludii-research-brief-spark-anvil-app-concepts.md`, 2026-09-04) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN standing program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the THIRD board-game-family brief after #112 (ADR-229, Report No. 74 board-games/mind-sports) + #113 (ADR-230, Report No. 75 board-game platform). Ludii = the Digital Ludeme Project's Java "general game system" (1,400+ traditional strategy games; an 809-concept self-play quality vocabulary; the 2026 CC0 *Ludus Coriovalli* rule-reconstruction). It COMPOSES/routes to #58 (younger board-game fit) · #86 (variants) · #107 (candidate disposition) · #100 (occasions) · #94 (VI heritage-language) · ADR-196/199/205 (engine corpus/bot/analysis infra) and FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. Eight hard invariants bind (ADR-235; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / dedup-HARD vs the LIVE fleet (origin/staging, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) — the roster is ~90% shipped, a re-proposal is recorded blue NEVER re-greened (the #117-caught-it-4× discipline; here ~9×); (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every green/app DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (concept-approval ≠ registry-add, R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything approved / prioritize it only" authorizes the PROGRAM not the spawns) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) CLEAN-ROOM legal hygiene — traditional/PD games only, authored from PRIMARY SOURCES, NEVER Ludii's `.lud`/prose/art, NEVER a modern designer game without permission, NEVER "Ludii" as OUR kid-facing mark (game rules are uncopyrightable "methods of operation" — Baker v. Selden / Copyright Office Compendium §910 / DaVinci Editrice; Ludii's CC BY-NC-ND no-derivatives wall never binds because we copy no expression); (4) MODE-by-default + naming (single-mechanic/roster games → mode/variant/younger-mode #58/#86/#107, never a duplicate app; clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS at spawn); (5) guardrails bind on any spawn (free/no-ads/no-accounts [except § `spark-anvil-website.md` R-OLDER-BAND-ACCOUNT-APPS for 13-18 board/puzzle]/on-device/COPPA · Socratic answer-withholding AI · anti-Gimkit · DN methodology · age-band register · 15-18 = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED [NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio] · cultural-sensitivity gates [cite sources · described-vs-observed rulesets · NAGPRA/UNDRIP register · R-SITE-NO-FOREIGN-FLAGS] · no new transport [Together rides the existing V261/ForgeTable ladder]); (6) HONEST-YIELD (a specific near-transfer skill / engagement / heritage-literacy / occasion, NEVER IQ / far-transfer / a proven learning-gain — Sala & Gobet 2017; the reconstruction mechanic = hypothesis-testing, never "deduces the truth", the scientists' own caveat); (7) ZERO paid gen (census+codify+track+guard; no repos/CONCEPT_SPAWN/Gemini) + site PRs=STAGING (ADR-154) / hub codification=main; (8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED (a future Ludii/general-game/heritage-game brief = a new #118 wave or #109 instance). THE LOAD-BEARING DECISION — the CLEAN-ROOM ENGINE (founder's "think harder" push, deep-researched): the "Anvil Ludeme Engine" is a founder-gated 🏗 INFRA/LIBRARY (NOT a reject, NOT a blind rebuild) — legally clean-room for traditional games, justified NOT by rebuilding the ~13 shipped per-game engines (the FCBM trap) but by the FOUR capabilities they can't provide (the reconstruction rule-space [prerequisite] · the design-forge composable rule-DSL · ~10× content velocity for future heritage games · the quality oracle); delivered as an R-LIBRARY-REPO-SPAWN scaffold (CubeKit pattern, not registry-added) + a web-first TS twin (hub owns the site, R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS) + a ForgeKit spec handoff for the Swift (hub NEVER writes Swift); reuse-referenced (OpenSpiel Apache-2 / boardgame.io MIT / Ludax Apache-2 — all reference-only, none on-device Swift/TS ludeme-DSL). Census (18 concepts, LIVE fleet 2026-09-04): 3 infra (anvil-ludeme-engine / ludii-quality-oracle / gametable-wg5-partnership) · 3🟢 (archaeoludology-reconstruction FLAGSHIP / clean-room-heritage-games / heritage-game-atlas) · 3🟡 (rule-forge-assay→#75 · agent-anvil→codeforge · pebble-caregiver-coplay→#117/#113/#58/DIR) · 10🔵 covered/roster · 0 auto-greenlights. Registered REVIEWED as a #109 instance (`REGISTRY_FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_COVERAGE.txt` via a `# CB-EXCLUDED:` line, the #112–#117 precedent). Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_ludii_concepts.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_LUDII_CONCEPTS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on a malformed row or an untracked build-worthy concept).** Founder-direct 2026-09-04 (*"create a new axis of the fable-reviewed concept mining program using the research brief 'ludii-research-brief-spark-anvil-app-concepts.md' … create a new program to track this work if needed. Prioritize it only … everything is approved. go with your recs. do not stop until fully done"* + *"what about creating a clean room implementation of the game engine and the actual games? deep web research and think harder"*). Decision `ADR-235`. Sibling of § R-BOARDGAME-MINDSPORT-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#112) · § R-BOARDGAME-PLATFORM-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#113) · § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (#35) · § R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING (#109, the composing parent); instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.
