## A VERTICAL-SPINE brief (one shared deterministic engine + one "vertical cast" aging across ALL bands + a cross-band saga + a localization axis + one app/band) is mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program that verdicts BOTH the SPINE ARTIFACTS and each per-band app — a distinct concept-SHAPE the flat #109 census can't hold (R-FABLE-VERTICAL-SPINE-MINING; 2026-09-05)

**Program #119 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program for VERTICAL-SPINE briefs — a NEW AXIS of the Fable-Reviewed Next-Level Elevation program (ADR-188 Amendment 13), the VERTICAL-SPINE sibling of § R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING (#109/ADR-226, flat independent concepts) and § R-FABLE-LEARNING-MATERIAL-* (#108). Its distinct UNIT of analysis is a portfolio LINE, not N independent apps: a single shared DETERMINISTIC engine + a "vertical cast" of characters that AGE across the age bands (the same character met at 4 and again at 17) + a cross-band shared saga + a first-localization axis, with one new app per band all sitting on that spine. So the Fable review adds SPINE-LEVEL verdict dimensions — each shared artifact (engine / vertical cast / localization / saga) is separately verdicted net-new-INFRA vs REUSE-existing, EVERY proposed cast name clears `distributed-narrative.md` § R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + the char registry + the DN swap-test (`R-DN-PARITY`), and the per-band app verdict turns on whether the shipped cluster already OWNS the spine's core primitive — on top of the per-band app census. It COMPOSES/FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116, and stays under the #74 umbrella. Eight hard invariants bind (ADR-236; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / DEDUP-HARD (spine-aware) vs the LIVE fleet (`apps.generated` origin/staging + active registry + per-clone `ageBand`, `R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH`), the brief's OWN claimed-overlap apps (does a shipped app already own the primitive? → 🔴/reuse), AND the concept programs — a primitive/engine already shipped is never a fresh 🟢; a short honest "mostly reuse / one real band-gap" is correct. (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every 🟢 (app OR net-new infra) DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (concept-approval ≠ registry-add, `workflow.md` § R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything approved / prioritize it only" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the spawns), fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; this axis produces verdicts (at most a `CONCEPT_SPAWN` doc), NEVER a repo. (3) MODE-by-default + naming (single-mechanic/partly-covered app → mode/feature/bundle; a shared engine that duplicates an existing one is a REUSE not a fresh package; clears the spawn-time naming rules). (4) GUARDRAILS bind — free/no-ads/no-accounts/no-PII/on-device/COPPA · the RENDERER is a DETERMINISTIC engine (on-device AI narrates/questions/plays-the-novice but NEVER draws the diagram or types the answer, the brief's §3.9 correctness-sensitive-visuals rule; `R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY`) · anti-Gimkit / `R-SITE-FEEDBACK` (3–8 = the WEEKLY co-play cadence, not daily streaks) · human-authored content · no foreign flags · DN methodology · age-band register. (5) HONEST-YIELD (a whitespace/linked-representation/co-play/differentiation argument, NEVER a proven-outcome claim — the brief's own g≈0.28 spatial-transfer + ST-Math-null honesty carry into the verdict). (6) ZERO paid gen in the review (census+verdicts only; Gemini unused). (7) site PRs=STAGING (ADR-154) / hub codification=main; Explore/export reads origin/staging (concept mining is hub-only → base=main, no site PR from the review). (8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED by construction — the guard auto-discovers spine briefs from a configurable external corpus (`$FABLE_VERTICAL_SPINE_DIR`) + committed bundles, and the corpus root + spine heuristic are configurable, so ANY vertical-spine brief in ANY domain is minable by the same tool + guard. Pipeline (all NEW, §5f-disjoint from #74/#106/#108/#109 tooling): `scripts/export_vertical_spine_bundle.py` → `Docs/nextlevel/_spine_briefs/<slug>/BUNDLE/` (`VERTICAL_SPINE_LENS.md` from `Docs/TEMPLATE_FABLE_VERTICAL_SPINE_PROMPT.md` + PATH-REFERENCED `MANIFEST.json`, ADR-047) → single-flight read-only Fable-5 Explore (`R-FABLE-SUBAGENT-SERIAL`) + Opus refute/verify → `Docs/nextlevel/_spine_briefs/<slug>/CONCEPTS_<slug>.md` (committed) → net-new verdicts (apps + infra) → `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`. Tracked + guarded: `scripts/check_fable_vertical_spine_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_FABLE_VERTICAL_SPINE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; per-brief tier NONE < BUNDLED < REVIEWED; `--ci-mode` fails only on a REVIEWED→lower regression; corpus-absent-safe). First instance (2026-09-05): the visual-math area-model spine brief (the viral (a+b+c)² tile → a 3–18 spine: AreaModelKit engine + the "Square Meadow" vertical cast + PatchPals 3–5 / ArrayPals 6–8 / IdentityForge 9–14 / InsightForge 15–18 + Vietnamese-first) — census in `Docs/nextlevel/_spine_briefs/visual-math-area-model-spine/CONCEPTS_visual-math-area-model-spine.md` (dedup-hard vs the shipped math cluster FractionForge/EquationQuest/GeometryForge/ProofQuest/MentalForge/InferForge/ShapePals/CountingPals).** Founder-direct 2026-09-05 (*"create a new axis of the fable-reviewed concept mining program using the research brief 'visual-math-area-model-spine.md' … create a new program to track this work if needed. Prioritize it only … everything is approved. go with your recs. do not stop until fully done … Codify and guard and track and generalize everything"*). Decision `ADR-236`. Sibling of § R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING (#109) · § R-LUDII-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#118) · § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM (#35, the destination pipeline); instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-236_FABLE_VERTICAL_SPINE_MINING.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_VISUAL_MATH_AREA_MODEL_SPINE_2026-09-05.md` (the imported brief) + `Docs/nextlevel/_spine_briefs/visual-math-area-model-spine/CONCEPTS_visual-math-area-model-spine.md` (the census verdict). OVERVIEW program #119.

(8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED — new site-design briefs auto-discover into the coverage registry from `$FABLE_SITE_DESIGN_BRIEF_DIR` + committed bundles; the export tooling + prompt template are domain-agnostic. §5b HEADLINE (Report-82): the brief audited PROD but `origin/staging` ALREADY implements most structural asks (lean 5-section splash · single-source counter `site.ts appCount` · left-drawer clone nav · age chooser · `/safety` · DTCG tokens) → DEDUP-heavy; ## An ORIGINAL-game-design brief (ludeme COMBINATION, not classic adaptation, AS the product) is mined as its own census-verdicted program (Report No. 83 "Win With Four, Lose With Three") — the 5th board-game-family brief + the STRONGEST §5b DEDUP of the series: ForgeLudeme→🔵 the shipped `_shared/ludeme.ts` (#118) · RuleForge/EvolveForge→🔵 the shipped Game-Forge/TableForge · classic recipes→🔵/🔴 #118/#112; the genuine net-new concentrates in the 3–5 BOARD-GAME HOLE (age 3–5 ships ZERO board games) + the WORD×BOARD middle Ludii can't model (no dictionary predicate) + the report-card-shipping-gate / ludeme-literacy / design-ladder POSTURES; every 🟢 founder-gated; honest-yield (novelty-as-combination + near-transfer of NAMED STRUCTURE, never far-transfer/IQ; every game is a HYPOTHESIS with a predicted failure — no human has played any) (R-LUDEME-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-07)

**Program #125 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the founder-supplied research brief Report No. 83 "Win With Four, Lose With Three" (`~/Downloads/Report-83-Win-With-Four-Lose-With-Three.md`, persisted `Docs/RESEARCH_LUDEME_ORIGINAL_GAME_DESIGN_2026-09-06.md`, 2026-09-06) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the 5TH board-game-family brief + the direct COMPANION of #118 (ADR-235, "Ludii, decoded" — the shipped ludemic engine) + #112 (ADR-229, Report No. 74) + #120 (ADR-242, BGA-vs-Ludii) + #114 (Report No. 76, words). It is the FIRST board-game-family brief to treat ORIGINAL game design — ludeme COMBINATION, not the adaptation of classics — AS the product, on Cameron Browne's Ludi/Yavalath thesis (novelty is cheap, quality is MEASURABLE: the interesting games live one rule away from the boring ones, and a machine culls 98% before a human plays). It proposes 16 original games (4/band: 3–5 FerryTots/NudgeTots/SoundTots/ChimeTots · 6–8 NudgePals/TagPals/LadderPals/FlipPals · 9–14 TangleForge/SpanForge/SowForge/RuleForge · 15–18 WhittleForge/AngelForge/SolveForge/EvolveForge) + a bench of 9 + the ForgeLudeme grammar (one rules language, two runtimes [Swift+TS], golden self-play tests, GridForge as the free regression corpus) + a design ladder (feel→name→play→compose→prove→publish→annual "Spark & Anvil Ludi") + a report-card shipping gate (Browne's completion/duration/decisiveness/drama/uncertainty/lead-change/killer-moves/first-move-advantage/branching + read-aloud/visible-win/explainable-loss) + a ludeme-literacy layer (recipe chips + `/ludemes` glossary + filter-by-ludeme) + the WORD×BOARD design space Ludii cannot model. It COMPOSES/routes to #118 (ForgeLudeme→the shipped `_shared/ludeme.ts` + RelicForge + the quality oracle) · the shipped `game-design-as-learning` edgame mode (= Game Forge) + TableForge (RuleForge/EvolveForge) · GridForge/AtlasTales/#112 (classic recipes) · LexiTales/WordForge/HintForge/CorpusForge + the Meaning Rule/#114 (the word substrate) · NimForge/NashForge (SolveForge/WhittleForge CGT) and FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. Eight hard invariants bind (ADR-244; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / DEDUP-HARD vs the LIVE fleet (origin/staging apps.generated.ts + the #118/#112/#120/#114 registries + REGISTRY_WEB_CLONES + per-clone ageBand, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) — this is the STRONGEST dedup of the series (ForgeLudeme/RuleForge/EvolveForge/classic-recipes are already shipped or mined → recorded 🔵/🔴, NEVER re-greened); a short honest "mostly reuse / a few real gaps" is the CORRECT outcome; (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every 🟢 (game OR net-new infra/feature/posture) DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (concept-approval ≠ registry-add, § `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything approved / prioritize it only" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the spawns) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) MODE-by-default + a grammar/engine that duplicates the shipped `_shared/ludeme.ts` is a REUSE not a new engine + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS (NEVER "Yavalath"/"boop"/"Letterpress"/"Hexicon"/"Ludii" on a kid surface) + the DN swap-test (R-DN-PARITY); (4) guardrails bind on any spawn (free/no-ads/no-accounts [except § `spark-anvil-website.md` R-OLDER-BAND-ACCOUNT-APPS for 13-18]/on-device/COPPA · the DETERMINISTIC engine adjudicates every rule AND renders the board — the LLM NEVER grades a move or draws [the report's own law; the AI is the novice the child teaches] · anti-Gimkit [3-5 = a no-fail/one-undo valve, not streaks] · DN methodology + cast + storybook · age-band register · 15-18 = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED text-forward NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio · cultural-sensitivity gates); (5) CLEAN-ROOM + CREDIT-CULTURE + OPEN-COMMONS hygiene (carries ADR-235, extended by the brief's §8.6 — build only from ludemes [rules are uncopyrightable methods-of-operation, Baker v. Selden / Compendium §910], NEVER a modern designer's rules-text/names/art without permission; do NOT ship Yavalath itself or reuse Browne's text; boop./Letterpress/Hexicon are trademarks credited-not-named; US 5,324,040 "bridge letters" lapsed 1998 = free; Hex/Ghost/mancala/word-ladders/the-angel-problem are PD/folklore; child-published RuleForge games carry a nickname+date+hash+CC BY-NC-SA — the proactive open-commons is a founder-gated POSTURE); (6) HONEST-YIELD (a whitespace/novelty-as-combination/near-transfer-of-NAMED-STRUCTURE/design-literacy/occasion argument — NEVER a proven learning-gain, NEVER far-transfer: the report itself says chess far-transfer fails active controls and what transfers is NAMED STRUCTURE [why the ludeme NAMES, not the games, are the payload]; making-games gains [Kafai & Burke; the Taiwanese SdBG study] are the honest ceiling; every game is a HYPOTHESIS with a predicted failure — "no human has played any"); (7) ZERO paid gen (census+codify+track+guard; no repos/CONCEPT_SPAWN/Gemini — gemini-available but UNUSED) + hub codification=main / site PRs=STAGING (ADR-154) / Explore reads origin/staging (hub-only mining → base=main, NO site PR from the review); (8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED (a future original-game-design / ludeme-grammar / game-design-tool brief = a new #125 wave or a #109 instance). One Fable-5 review (single-flight, read-only — R-FABLE-SUBAGENT-SERIAL) audits the LIVE fleet + census-verdicts each of the 16 games + 9 bench + ForgeLudeme + design-ladder + report-card + ludeme-literacy layer; Opus refutes + verifies vs origin/staging. Prioritize it only … everything is approved. go with your recs. do not stop until fully done … describe and explain in detail each option/wave before you start … Codify and guard and track and generalize everything. gemini key is available. explore agent uses site staging, not main. always open PRs against site staging, not main."*). ## Roblox-lens creation-engine briefs are mined for concepts via a census-verdicted program (Report No. 86 "The Lab That Left the Classroom") — the DISTINCT lens none of the prior briefs carried is the CREATION ENGINE (a child builds a place another child walks through) + the avatar-SELF + the shared real-time space + the persistent world-state + the economy-literacy of crates/timers/take-rates; the census is HIGH-green (the CREATION GAP is genuine whitespace across all 303 live apps — the mirror of #126's deal gap + #128's mechanism gap, 0 red/blue); the crucial §5b CATCH is the ForgeAvatar name-collision (the brief's proposed engine collides with the SHIPPED ForgeKit ForgeAvatar module → reuse/extend, recommend ForgeSelf); every 🟢/🏗 founder-gated; borrow Roblox's FORMS + Minecraft's FILE (the Cartridge Rule + the Timer Rule); honest-yield (creation as the front door / the couch-tonight motivation / on-device trust, never a proven learning-gain or the young-entrepreneur pitch) (R-CREATION-ENGINE-CONCEPT-PROGRAM; 2026-09-07)

**Program #129 is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the founder-supplied Roblox-in-2026 brief Report No. 86 "The Lab That Left the Classroom" (4 shared engines + 2 Method-page laws + 16 primary + 9 bench app concepts across 4 bands) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the 9th such instance. The brief brackets Roblox as THREE things wearing one blocky avatar — a CREATION ENGINE (Studio + Luau + Cube + a first-class MCP server, descended in a straight line per the company's own S-1 from a 1989 classroom physics sim, Interactive Physics), a SOCIAL PLATFORM (a 2025-26 face-scan bureaucracy that shed ~20M users under 170+ MDL cases + $50.8M in state settlements), and a TWO-PRICE ECONOMY (the maker sees ~a quarter of the dollar; the median DevEx creator earned ~$1,500 vs a top-1,000 average of ~$1.3M) — and its organizing finding is that CHILDREN LOVE THE FIRST THING AND TOLERATE THE OTHER TWO, AND THE PORTFOLIO HAS NONE OF THE FIRST THING. It COMPOSES/FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116 (#35), and its DISTINCT LENS the sibling briefs never carried is the creation engine itself: the child as MAKER of a playable place, the avatar-SELF, the shared real-time space, the persistent world, and the economy-literacy of the mechanics a 7-12-year-old actually meets. Eight hard invariants bind (ADR-248; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / DEDUP-HARD vs the LIVE fleet (origin/staging apps.generated.ts, 303 apps, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) AND the concept programs — even in a high-green census the discipline separates the genuine gap from already-covered adjacency (LevelForge design-reasoning / ChanceForge probability / NexusForge stock-and-flow / GrowForge+SproutLab garden science / SignalForge meme spread / SafetyForge be-safe), a re-proposal recorded 🟡/🔵 never re-greened; (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every 🟢 (app/infra/posture) DEFERRED + founder-gated per-title (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT: "everything approved / prioritize it only" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the spawns; concept-approval ≠ registry-add) logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt` + fed to `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`; (3) MODE-by-default + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS (NEVER "Roblox"/"Robux"/"Studio"/a brainrot character on a kid surface; "obby" is community-generic but never the only word on the tin) + the DN swap-test (R-DN-PARITY); (4) guardrails bind on any spawn — free/no-ads/**no-accounts/no-servers/no-chat**/on-device/COPPA · the CARTRIDGE RULE (a world is a FILE not a server listing; borrow Roblox's forms + Minecraft's file) · the TIMER RULE (no purchasable wait, nothing rots — no streaks/daily-rewards/rotating-shops/reminders; anti-Gimkit / R-SITE-FEEDBACK; extends the Pip Rule to crates) · same-room or by-code play with a known person only (rides the V261/ForgeTable ladder ADR-041/ADR-160, never a new transport, never a stranger) · Socratic answer-withholding AI (the cast are novices the child teaches; R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY) · DN methodology + cast + storybook · age-band register · 15-18 = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED text-forward NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio (StudioDojo/PlatformForge/FilterForge/SyncForge); (5) 🔴 §5b CATCH — the ForgeAvatar infra-dedup: the brief's proposed engine "ForgeAvatar" COLLIDES with the SHIPPED ForgeKit `ForgeAvatar` module (ADR-022 — AvatarConfig/AvatarRenderer/AvatarStudioView/ForgeAvatarRegistry) → the avatar-self engine REUSES/EXTENDS it (recommend name ForgeSelf), exactly like #128 ForgeCampaign→legacyCampaign (ADR-119) + #126 ForgeGraph→forgeHex; ForgeWorld/ForgeCartridge/ForgeScript have NO ForgeKit collision → genuinely net-new LIBRARIES (R-LIBRARY-REPO-SPAWN); (6) HONEST-YIELD — creation as the front door / "someone you love will play the thing you made tonight on the couch" / on-device-no-account trust / economy-literacy at the age the child is inside the economy, NEVER a proven learning-gain / IQ / far-transfer / young-entrepreneur claim (the brief itself: the ~42,000 DevEx participants against hundreds of millions of accounts = a crossing rate well under 0.1%; (3) MODE-by-default + naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS + the DN swap-test — **NEVER "Alpha"/"Timeback"/"Incept"/"2 Hour Learning"/"Floortime"/"Greenspan"/"DIR"/"Hassabis"/"DeepMind" on a kid surface** (trademark + real-person names belong in a parents' essay, never a chapter — Brief A §VIII). (4) guardrails bind on any spawn — free/no-ads/no-accounts/on-device/COPPA · answer-withholding Socratic mentor (never a bot that closes every circle — a mentor that answers removes the human partner from the loop, Brief A §VIII / PNAS-2025) · anti-Gimkit — the Given-Back clock is **NEVER a streak**, and NO camera/keystroke/eye-tracking/XP-per-minute/cash/prizes (Alpha's forbidden set, Brief B §8.2/8.3; R-SITE-FEEDBACK) · DN methodology + cast · age-band register · 15-18 = R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED text-forward, NO illustrated chapters/portraits/audio (SigmaForge/GuideForge/SprintForge/TwoDeep/FutureForge/AgentForge/FrontierLab). (5) **MARQUEE GUARDRAIL — NEVER-MARKET-THE-PRODIGY / NEVER "2×/10×/top-1%"** (honest-yield, from BOTH briefs) — DIR's own rule is that *when* a skill is learned matters far less than how well; Alpha's "2.6×" is a marketing artefact that cost it credibility; every claim carries its prior, the pilot is pre-registered (Brief B §17.2 — own-words + a two-week retention re-check, effect sizes with CIs and n, no growth-multiple → the efficacy lane R4/ADR-061 + #190's "What we don't claim yet"). (6) NO new transport — Sprint Rooms / Play-Together ride the V261/ForgeTable ladder (ADR-041/ADR-160); regulate-before-challenge is on-device fatigue/error-burst detection, NO biometric capture. (7) ZERO paid gen (census+codify+track+guard; Gemini available but UNUSED) + hub codification base=`main` / any downstream site build (a web-first `/play` kernel, R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS) base=`staging` (ADR-154) / Explore reads origin/staging (hub-only mining → NO site PR from the review; the circles-per-session metric posture feeds the #190 /method-page track base=staging). (8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED (a future developmental-learning / AI-microschool / mastery-environment brief = a new #198 wave or a fresh #109 instance). GENUINE NET-NEW payload (founder-gated): 🏗 **MasteryKit spine** = HonestSprint (age-scaled 5/15/25-min timer, cast-led break, stop-without-penalty, NO streak) + the **Given-Back clock** (time-returned = published baseline − minutes-to-mastery, shown only after mastery, tappable arithmetic, never a streak) — Alpha's ONE load-bearing mechanic the fleet lacks AS A SINGLE SPINE; R-LIBRARY-REPO-SPAWN; composes #121 Comfortable-Start/Ritual/PacketPals/LadderForge, anti-Gimkit HARD (the antidote to Alpha's XP/waste-meter, not a re-skin) · 🟢 SprintPals (6-8 flagship) · 🟢 OddBitsPals (3-5 broken-toy-authors-a-rule) · 🟢 SecondLookForge (second-look reflex, distinct from #93 POE) · 🟢 circles-per-session/ideas-not-minutes metric (POSTURE → #190). Census (LIVE 468-clone fleet, 2026-09-20): **1 infra + 4 green + 18 yellow + 13 blue · 0 red · 0 auto-greenlights.** Registered REVIEWED as a #109 instance (`REGISTRY_FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_COVERAGE.txt` via a `# CB-EXCLUDED:` line). **Program #203** is the standing, census-verdicted concept program mining the two founder-supplied 2026-09-24 Whetstone briefs (`~/Downloads/Whetstone `, persisted `Docs/RESEARCH_GROWNUP_COMMONS_WHETSTONE_2026-09-24.md` [the research brief] + `Docs/RESEARCH_GROWNUP_COMMONS_ADR_PACK_2026-09-24.md` [9 Wave-0 ADRs]) — a census-verdicted INSTANCE of Program #109 (ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING), tracked as its OWN program (R-GROUP-RELATED-INTO-PROGRAMS) and the **29TH concept-brief instance + the FIRST brief proposing a NET-NEW THIRD SIBLING SITE for a NET-NEW AUDIENCE (adults)** — "Whetstone / the grown-up commons" (parents/grandparents/lifelong learners), assembled from S&A's shipped genome (6 pillars Today/Play/Words/Learn/Together/Shelf · 7 adult design laws #16-22 · a "Great Courses × active learning" catalog · intergenerational connected play). Its DISTINCT LENS: a NEW sibling site for a NEW audience (now live), where #101/#196/#199/#201 mine the existing FC site. It COMPOSES/FEEDS (never bypasses) § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT + ADR-116. Eight hard invariants bind (ADR-291; each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / DEDUP-HARD vs the shipped S&A genome + the concept programs (R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) — the census is **DEDUP-HEAVY by design** (the brief's own §1 "you already have the genome"): ~22 building blocks are ALREADY SHIPPED on S&A (the covered rows of `REGISTRY_GROWNUP_COMMONS_CONCEPTS.txt`) → recorded COVERED, NEVER re-greened; (2) **FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — 0 new S&A app concepts → NEVER `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`** (the #199 precedent; the unit is a whole new SITE, not an S&A app-count-ceiling entry); (3) REUSE/COMPOSE-by-default (the adult site is a THIRD RAIL of the shipped engines + the cross-site prerequisite ladder); naming clears § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE (domain live; USPTO + App Store name = founder-account); (4) GUARDRAILS bind on any build — free/no-ads/no-tracking (the Patron/business-model exception IS the master decision) · **answer-withholding no-companion mentor** (R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY, CA SB 243 / NY) · **no-feed** (Bottom Rule + Apple's Sept-2026 social-media-capabilities questionnaire answered No) · anti-Gimkit (R-SITE-FEEDBACK) · accessibility floor · **any room containing a child runs entirely under S&A's chat-free nickname-only rules** (the intergenerational bridge RIDES the shipped ADR-041 rooms → NO new S&A safety surface, the brief's key insight); (5) **HONEST-YIELD — the No-Fear clause is the marquee**: a richer inner life / better at chess / an hour well spent, NEVER a memory/dementia/IQ/brain-age claim (Lumosity $2M FTC 2016; Sala & Gobet 2017 no far transfer; ASPREE 2023 "associated" only); (6) ZERO paid gen (Gemini available but UNUSED — a PURE CENSUS PROGRAM, the #126/#128/#196/#199 precedent); (7) POSTURE — hub codification base=`main` (this review touches NO site files → NO site PR); any future adult-site build is its OWN site repo base=`staging` (ADR-154); Explore/reads use origin/staging; Swift = a ForgeKit handoff (hub never writes Swift); (8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED — a future adult-site / continuing-learning / new-sibling-site brief = a new #203 wave or a fresh #109 instance. **THE MASTER GATE**: the whole program blocks on TWO founder decisions — **whetstone-third-site** (stand up a third adult sibling site, y/n — the biggest portfolio-shape decision) + **business-model** (ADR-003: free-forever [A] vs optional **Patron membership** [B, recommended] vs freemium [C]; OVERVIEW program #203.

### R-CENSUS-REGISTRY-LIFECYCLE (2026-09-29)

**A census registry whose program BUILDS must record delivery: a delivered status (`shipped|partial|handoff-filed`) + `evidence:<PR/route>` per row — else it freezes while the product ships. Tripwire `check_census_registry_lifecycle.py` (baseline 0). Axis (C, P#205 §S12): a census row MUST NOT read not-built while the program's own per-concept build registry marks it `shipped`. A deferral waiting only on shipped rows fails `concept_census` (j). → ADR-291 §Amd11.** **R-PROGRAM-INBOUND-ROUTES:** receivers run `check_program_inbound_routes.py --program N` at close-out.

### R-NEW-SITE-FLEET-GUARD-ENROLLMENT (2026-10-01)

**A new hub-owned prod website is enrolled in the fleet guards the day it goes live: a prod-monitor row (R-PROD-MONITORING) and an in-repo dependency allowlist gate (R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN). A guard written for one site sees only that site. Guard `check_family_site_guard_enrollment.py` + `REGISTRY_FAMILY_SITES.txt` (`--online`: headers, CSP, Cloudflare Web Analytics on with the EU included; fails an inline script the live per-page CSP meta doesn't hash). Prove a CSP on the LIVE site (edge-injected scripts appear only there) as Report-Only on pages + driven features (site `scripts/csp-probe-{live,drive}.mjs`). A per-page script-hash meta goes right after a first-in-`<head>` charset, restates `worker-src`, and is re-sealed after any HTML rewrite. → ADR-291 §Amd18–22.** **R-SISTER-SITE-DEEPLINK-PROD:** sister-site links into S&A resolve on PROD, route + query keys; `check_sister_site_deeplinks.py`.

It COMPOSES (never re-litigates) its two direct near-twins — #186 (ADR-267, the reimagining *container* lens, D1 set complete) + #93 (ADR-208, the *same* Theme Park source: R-THEME-PARK-SIM-LOOP-MECHANICS + the tracked crowd-sim-loop/CrowdForge) — and the site-design MOVE-mining sibling #124 (ADR-243). Eight hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) §5b VERIFY-BEFORE-PROPOSE / DEDUP-HARD vs the LIVE fleet (site origin/staging, 495 clones, R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) AND the concept programs — DEDUP-HEAVY (the near-twin class). TWO #1-FCBM catches: ParkForge/Little-People-Lab (§8.13) → 🔵 covered-concept = the already-assessed crowd-sim-loop/CrowdForge (#93/ADR-208, CONCEPT_SPAWN_CROWDFORGE), NOT re-greened; the web-"ForgeKit" engine stack (§6–7) → 🔴 NAME-COLLISION with the shipped iOS SPM ForgeKit (+ forgeplay taken) → RENAME at any build. (2) FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling — every net-new move DEFERRED + founder-gated per-move ("everything approved" authorizes the PROGRAM, not the ~15 build/adoption executions; R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT); logged in REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt. (3) MOVE-by-default, NOT a new app; any name at build clears distributed-narrative.md R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED + R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE + R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS (no branded parks/mascots/licensed rides) + the DN swap-test. (4) the net-new INFRA is founder+ADR SDK-gated — the Character Contract depends on Rive (net-new dep); the engine-component stack adds Pixi/GSAP/Rive/Phaser/Rapier (Three ^0.170 is ALREADY a lazy dep per ADR-212/251); adopting any is a founder+ADR decision (R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE + R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN); full engines (Godot/Unity web export) REJECTED (R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS: no SharedArrayBuffer/COOP-COEP + R-SITE-PAGE-WEIGHT-BUDGET); HARD: a Rive cast face stays NON-conversational (`speaking`=authored-audio-timing, never generated dialogue — R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY / CA SB 243). (5) GUARDRAILS bind on any build — free/no-ads/no-accounts/on-device/COPPA · the "calm juice" is feedback for the ACT not for RETURNING (the banned half [confetti-for-login, streaks, screen-shake, loot, countdowns, push] is already R-SITE-FEEDBACK/Calm Rule) · reduced-motion keeps the MEANING · WCAG 2.2 AA both schemes · dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD · age-band (Tots=toys, 15-18=R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED) · TIER-1 for a shared-shell/SDK move (adversarial + founder VISUAL gate). (6) HONEST-YIELD (never a proven learning-gain; Genie/world-models rejected) · (7) ZERO paid gen, hub base=main, any downstream site build base=staging · (8) ONGOING + GENERALIZED — detail → ADR-292. Census (25 moves, 12 net-new, 3 infra, 0 conflicts) + the genuine net-new core → ADR-292 § Census. Registered REVIEWED as a #109 instance (REGISTRY_FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_COVERAGE.txt via a # CB-EXCLUDED: line). (3) THE PRIORITY AXIS COMPOSES, never duplicates: D1 engine-priority = adopt the engine that MAXIMIZES the learning/playing experience quality per surface (the retired low-power floor no longer caps the choice; page-weight/LCP/a11y/offline stay QUALITY gates, not low-power gates; the brief's rung-ladder + Anvil-Kernel are the codified reference) → COMPOSES § R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE + Program #204 (ADR-292/AnvilFeel), dep adoptions stay founder-ratified-allowlist (R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN), NOT a rival engine program; D2 low-power-removal = RE-AFFIRM the founder's fresh explicit re-issue against ADR-293 (floor retired + WebGPU baseline + mt-WASM approved) + ADR-294 (rejection class approved) + ADR-297 (mt-WASM = online default + low-end tier dropped; the last codified TODAY), the residual CORE-flip EXECUTION (numThreads flip + per-route COOP/COEP + real-device COI visual gate + threaded Stockfish + founder CF-preview re-enable) = the device+founder-gated q-p297-full-multithread (NOT headlessly firable; R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA), NOT a duplicate removal. (4) guardrails bind (free/no-ads/no-accounts/on-device/COPPA · solver-verified no-guess/winnable fairness · Why-Coach EXPLAINS-never-solves R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY · anti-Gimkit R-SITE-FEEDBACK · never-landmines-for-3-8 R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS · DN cast R-DN-PARITY · 15-18 R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED · web-first R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS · Swift=ForgeKit handoff · naming NEVER "Minesweeper/Solitaire/Klondike/Dino/Wordament/Champion-Island/Doodle" R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS). (5) HONEST-YIELD — engagement/ritual/fairness DESIGN + a specific deduction/probability/sequencing/statistics skill, NEVER far-transfer/IQ; the engine-priority axis = experience QUALITY not a proven outcome. (6) ZERO paid gen (Gemini UNUSED) + hub codify base=main / any downstream /play kernel PR base=staging (ADR-154) / Explore reads origin/staging — a PURE CENSUS program. (7) ONGOING + GENERALIZED. (#218) "Three Doors" app discovery is MOVE-mining (the #124/#199 shape): moves feed #74 base=staging, never the candidate registry; "know the apps deeply, know the child only on the child's device" (device log, reset is real, no feed/autoplay/streaks); three conflicts split + deferred (moving card loop vs R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER, four-row home vs R-SITE-3HUB-IA, Plausible events vs the privacy promise). ## A younger-band (3-8) version of a board game is FIT-ASSESSED, not blanket-spawned — chess is SPECIAL (piece-types×rules = character map); MODE-by-default, standalone-app-rare, founder-gated (R-YOUNGER-BOARD-GAME-FIT; 2026-08-21)

**A younger-band (3-8) version of a board game is a per-app FIT-ASSESSMENT, never a blanket fan-out: chess is special (six piece-types, six rules, a 1:1 character map), most games are not. Default for a real fit = a younger MODE in the existing app; a standalone younger app is rare and founder-gated per title (PawnPals so far). Verdicts 🟢 standalone / 🟡 mode / 🔴 no-fit. ### R-YOUNGER-BOARD-GAME-MODE — building a 🟡 candidate's younger MODE is a tracked "fit ≠ built" drain (2026-08-25)

**BUILDING a 🟡 candidate's younger MODE is a tracked "fit ≠ built" fan-out drain. A younger MODE = a NEW ages-6–8 route INSIDE the existing clone (no new repo/count-bump) teaching the game's core in the younger register: AUDIO-FIRST (`_shared/speech`), BIG targets (`ageBand:'6-8'` CloneNav + `.<clone>-younger` CSS; R-YOUNGER-BAND-LAYOUT-NAV), SHORT/RHYMING R-DN-PARITY-true copy (R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER), reachable (R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE), dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD — REUSING the clone's shipped engine+learn surface, NEVER a rebuild. Per-clone bespoke (games differ) → reference-first (crowntales `/little` "Little Crowns", SHIPPED site #2763). A 🟡 candidate with NEITHER a `mode:` row NOR a `# MODE-EXCLUDED:` line is silently-forgotten. Tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_YOUNGER_BOARD_GAME_MODE.txt` + `scripts/check_younger_board_game_mode_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B). Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS; STC sibling of `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-STC-FEATURE-FANOUT.**

## Every assessed board-game CANDIDATE reaches a FINAL disposition in a self-checking registry — an assessed-but-unbuilt candidate must never be lost in prose; and a "fold game X into bundle Y" claim is re-checked against Y's IDENTITY (board-shape + primitive-family) before assuming it folds (R-BOARD-GAME-CANDIDATE-DISPOSITION; 2026-09-01)

**Every board-game CANDIDATE ever assessed MUST carry a `cand:` row in `Docs/REGISTRY_BOARD_GAME_CANDIDATE_DISPOSITION.txt` with a FINAL `verdict` (green/yellow/blue/red) + `disposition` (standalone-app | web-clone:<slug> | mode:<app> | variant:<app> | covered:<app> | reject) + `status` — because the board-game program tracked feature-PARITY + younger-FIT of BUILT apps but had NO registry for assessed-but-unbuilt CANDIDATES, so a 🟡 candidate not spawned immediately lived only in a dated research doc and was SILENTLY FORGOTTEN (Chinese Checkers is the proof). A `status=assessed/deferred` row with no final build call is a WARN; an assessed candidate with NO row is the silent-forget it forbids. THREE load-bearing sub-rules: (1) a "fold game X into bundle Y" call is REFUTED unless X matches Y's IDENTITY on BOTH axes — board-shape AND primitive-family (the ADR-109 test: it made Nine-Men's-Morris a standalone `millsong`, and refutes "Chinese Checkers = gridforge sibling"); a candidate folding into NO bundle earns a standalone, its web-first `/play` clone proceeds (`R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS`) while a standalone iOS app-spawn stays founder-gated (`R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT`). (2) a candidate's disposition is FLEET-VERIFIED against live `origin/staging` surfaces BEFORE trusting the inherited prose verdict — a stale `assessed`/`unbuilt` row routinely turns out ALREADY-COVERED (15-puzzle was shipped in `puzzleforge/slidingtile.ts`; the `R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH` trap) → `covered:<app>`, never a redundant build. (3) an existing game's VARIANT (janggi→xiangqi) feeds § R-BOARD-GAME-VARIANT-SUPPORT (#86), never a duplicate app. Guard `scripts/check_board_game_candidate_disposition.py` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on a malformed row or a `status=shipped` disposition drifting off the feature registry). Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS; FEEDS § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT (never bypasses the ceiling); sibling of § R-INNOVATIVE-EDGAME-PROGRAM · § R-YOUNGER-BOARD-GAME-FIT · § R-BOARD-GAME-VARIANT-SUPPORT.** Founder-direct 2026-09-01/-09-02; decision `ADR-224`; research `RESEARCH_CHINESE_CHECKERS_FLEET_FIT_2026-09-01.md` + `RESEARCH_BOARD_GAME_CANDIDATE_TAIL_2026-09-02.md`; OVERVIEW #107.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-224_CHINESE_CHECKERS_FLEET_SUPPORT.md` + `Docs/CONCEPT_SPAWN_LEAPTALE_2026-09-01.md`.

## Taking an app/clone to the "next level" runs through the standing FABLE review loop — export a docs+screenshots BUNDLE → a Fable-model review returns a guardrail-fit prioritized PLAN → hub ingests/dedupes/executes (PRs→site staging); every surface has a bundle+plan OR a documented exclusion (R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW; 2026-08-24)

**"Take app/clone X to the next level" is NOT an ad-hoc redesign — it runs through the standing 5-wave Fable-Reviewed Next-Level Elevation loop (Program #74): (W1) `export_nextlevel_bundle.py` writes `Docs/nextlevel/<app>/BUNDLE/` = curated docs + dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshots + the guardrail-encoded `ANALYSIS_PROMPT.md`; (W2) a FABLE-model session — in-session `model: fable` subagent (ADR-188 Amd 1), **dispatched ONE Fable subagent AT A TIME, never a parallel batch (R-FABLE-SUBAGENT-SERIAL, founder-direct 2026-08-27, ADR-188 Amd 7 — heavy same-account subagents fan-out-cap; sibling of `workflow.md` § R-WEBSEARCH-FANOUT-RATELIMIT)** — or founder-run — returns `NEXTLEVEL_PLAN_<app>.md` in the canonical schema; (W3) `ingest_nextlevel_plan.py` normalizes it and hub §5b-DEDUPES each rec against the existing programs BEFORE filing a `program:74` queue item (re-chasing already-covered work is the defect); (W4) execute worst-first cluster-priority round-robin, screenshot-DoD verdict, skins via single-flight Gemini, features via shared-shell + engine-verified. Three load-bearing invariants: the canonical prompt's HARD guardrails (`TEMPLATE_FABLE_NEXTLEVEL_ANALYSIS_PROMPT.md`: no-dark-patterns · on-device/COPPA · Socratic answer-withholding AI · quiet-stage · WCAG-2.2-AA-both-schemes · age-band · honest-yield · web-only `/play` recs) ARE the quality gate; ALL site PRs base=`staging` (never main, ADR-154; explore reads `origin/staging`); a clone at tier NONE with no `# EXCLUDED:` line is a tracked gap. **IMMERSION IS A PREREQUISITE (founder-direct 2026-08-24): a `/play` clone WITHOUT the ChessKid immersion treatment (`immersive: true` in its `nav.ts`) is `DEFERRED` — its Fable review is SKIPPED until it is immersed (a newer, un-immersed clone would only draw "add the immersion treatment" recs, already the § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT + ChessKid Immersion Backfill program). The guard auto-detects this from the live staging flag and auto re-eligibles the clone once immersion lands — never a hand-list; site surfaces are exempt.** GENERALIZES the proven Program-#71 Fable-5 batch (ADR-185) into a self-checking fleet pipeline.** Founder-direct 2026-08-24; decision `ADR-188`; tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_FABLE_NEXTLEVEL_COVERAGE.txt` + `scripts/check_fable_nextlevel_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN). Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS; ordering per `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PEDAGOGICAL-PRIORITY; external-lens rationale per `workflow.md` § R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA. **Workflow v2 (evidence-hardened, ADR-188 Addendum): the prompt CONTEXT-GROUNDS Fable in the in-bundle parity/forward-port docs → propose ONLY net-new (dedup at source); 3-lens review THEN an independent in-session-Opus adversarial-REFUTE pass; task-flow storyboard + a11y tree (not one hero shot; interaction concerns = UNVERIFIED, founder visual gate final); ingest runs a DETERMINISTIC guardrail-LINT (never trust Fable's self-cert) + a RICE score (Confidence penalizes speculative recs) sorted within cluster-priority. Never single-shot-trust a self-certified static-screenshot review. **UI/UX is a FIRST-CLASS, MANDATORY review dimension** (founder-direct 2026-08-24): every review assesses EVERY surface against the full UI/UX axis set — visual hierarchy/prominence · layout & responsiveness (panel-fit · board-top-flush · mobile breakpoints) · spacing/density (8pt scale · visual-first) · color scheme & DARK MODE (AA both schemes) · immersion/skin (boundary-only, quiet stage) · interaction/controls (≥44px · drag-alt · dual-pointer) · WCAG 2.2 AA — with a dedicated `ui-ux` rec type, screenshot-DoD as the verdict, `GUIDE_WEB_CLONE_UI_UX_BEST_PRACTICES` + the shipped UI/UX rule set as the bar.** **Site-surface track (founder-direct 2026-08-24, ADR-188 Addendum 2): the program ALSO covers the SITE itself via THREE bundles (`--site-surface`/`--all-site`), each a distinct research-grounded review lens — (1) `site-homepage` = first-impression/clarity; (2) `site-content-hubs` (`/play`+`/story`+`/books`) = IA/findability, reviewed together for cross-hub consistency; (3) `site-marketing` = nonprofit trust/credibility/transparency + the AI-safety moat. Each bundle carries a generated `SITE_BRIEF.md` (lens + load-bearing SITE rules to reconcile against, not a parity ledger); the 3 `site-*` surfaces are tracked in the coverage guard; site-surface recs are higher-blast (shared `Hub*`/chrome) → single-flight + R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA.**

**AMENDMENTS (brief `v5`; ADR-188 Amd 2–3, founder-direct 2026-08-25) — the load-bearing invariants:** (1) **TWO-TRACK output** — every Fable review returns BOTH the prioritized critique AND a generative UI/UX **DESIGN PROPOSAL** (with `MOCK: desktop`+`MOCK: mobile` renderable mockups → hub renders 4 dark-safe PNGs). 🔒 **ADR-100 Pillar 2**: a proposal is a SPEC + throwaway visualization — Claude Code implements it into the real `_shared/`/`Hub*`; NO design-tool/AI-design-to-code output ships, the mockup markup is never lifted verbatim; reuse-first. (2) **SUBJECT taxonomy widens** beyond per-surface to three META/SYSTEM subjects (curated bundle + `SUBJECT_BRIEF` + two-track, tracked immersion-exempt): `theme-system`, `immersion-system`, `prompt-fable-nextlevel` (SELF-review of the program's own prompt — generalizes to any prompt; mocks N/A → proposal = a REVISED TEMPLATE; hub adversarially refutes its own-brief review). (3) **per-CLONE theme-fit + immersion-fit are MANDATORY** review dimensions. Tooling `export_nextlevel_bundle.py --subject/--all-subjects` + `render_nextlevel_mockups.mjs`; fleet hygiene = `capture_nextlevel_screenshots.mjs --all` (gitignored) + `export … --all*` re-render (own PR).

**AMENDMENT — Fable-review recs FAN OUT to the fleet; LIFT what generalizes (Program #75, founder-direct 2026-08-25).** A Fable-review rec is very often NOT one surface's problem — a **shared-shell defect**, a **fleet-wide UI/UX pattern**, or a **reusable feature/skin** surfaced on one reviewed surface GENERALIZES across the portfolio. Such a rec MUST be **LIFTED and FANNED OUT to the rest of the fleet**, not fixed only on its origin surface — a generalizable rec applied origin-only is a **silently-forgotten fleet gap** (`R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS`). At §5b (verify-before-act, fresh origin) classify each rec's SCOPE — `shared-shell` (ONE single-flight fix fans to every adopter, TIER-1 `R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER`) · `fleet-pattern` (a worst-first per-clone drain) · `reusable-feature`/`reusable-skin` (adopt where archetype-appropriate) · `cluster` · `not-generalizable` (execute per #74, recorded so the call is explicit) — and "lift what makes sense" (NEVER a padded/forced fan-out; honest-yield). Fan-out reuses the immersion-fanout cluster-parallel machinery (`R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT`), worst-first by pedagogical cluster priority (`R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PEDAGOGICAL-PRIORITY`), site PRs base=`staging`, `_WEB_BACKPORT` for iOS (hub never writes Swift), and a rec conflicting with a prior founder-direct is split+flagged not silently fanned (`workflow.md` § R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT). This is the fan-out sibling of #74 exactly as #18 immersion-fanout is of #17. TRACKED + GUARDED: `Docs/REGISTRY_FABLE_REC_FANOUT.txt` + `scripts/check_fable_rec_fanout_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN — surfaces every generalizable rec not-yet-`done`); queue `q-fable-rec-fanout`. Reference lifts: gambittales v5 R1/R6/R7 → fleet lanes q-acbed9/q-268706.

**AMENDMENT — a review BUNDLE + its SCREENSHOTS are a POINT-IN-TIME SNAPSHOT that STALES as PRs merge; RE-EXPORT + RE-CAPTURE against fresh origin/staging immediately before dispatching a review, and §5b-VERIFY every ACTED-ON rec against fresh origin — never trust the bundle (R-FABLE-BUNDLE-FRESHNESS; founder-flagged 2026-08-26).** A `BUNDLE/` (its `MANIFEST.json` `"generated"` stamp + STEP-0 doc snapshots + captured screenshots) reflects the site/hub state AT EXPORT TIME. A day of merged site PRs (staging) or hub PRs staleness it → a review run on a stale bundle flags ALREADY-CLOSED gaps and MISSES recent merges (the "builds green, reviews stale" class). Two invariants, each a defect: (1) **before dispatching W2**, re-run `export_nextlevel_bundle.py … --stamp <today>` (refreshes STEP-0 doc snapshots + stamp) and — for any surface whose recs depend on pixels — re-run `capture_nextlevel_screenshots.mjs` against **origin/staging** (R-SITE-WORKTREE); a DOCS-ONLY bundle skips the capture but still re-exports; (2) **before ACTING on any rec** (the #75 fan-out or the #74 build), §5b-VERIFY it against FRESH `origin/staging` (Explore reads staging) — the bundle is a lead, fresh origin is the oracle (this is what refuted immersion-system R3: the "silent-failure" defect did not reproduce on fresh source). Sibling of § R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH + `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-GEN-DRAIN-VERIFY-R2-GROUND-TRUTH + § R-SITE-WORKTREE. GUARD: `check_fable_nextlevel_coverage.py` WARNs on a bundle whose `generated` stamp is older than `Docs/nextlevel/<app>/NEXTLEVEL_PLAN` / a freshness horizon (a stale bundle carrying a PLANNED plan = re-export-before-trust).

**AMENDMENT — GEN-ASSET subject class: ALL Gemini gens are Fable-reviewable (founder-direct 2026-08-27, "add cast character portraits, book covers, basically ALL* gemini gens to the fable-reviewed design elevation program").** The Fable review's SUBJECT taxonomy adds a `gen-asset` class covering EVERY Gemini-generated kid-facing asset — cast portraits · mascots · book covers (all formats) · chapter-beat art/openers/spots · app icons · topic/backdrop/modecard illustrations. A `gen-asset` review bundle = the RENDERED art (a per-app or per-batch set; scheme-independent) + the gen PROMPT/style + the appropriateness/quality CRITERIA as the bar: **R-CHARACTER-ATTIRE-DIGNITY (ADR-195 — neat/whole clothing · both legs visible · no unintended peg-leg/stump/hook/missing-limb caricature)** · R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS · R-ANATOMY-GATE · R-PATH-B-TEXT-LEAK-GATE · R-SPAWN-REUSE-ILLUSTRATION-STYLE (style-consistency) · R-DN-PARITY (depiction matches the character's canonical role/form) · R-SITE-NO-FOREIGN-FLAGS · next-level polish. Fable returns a prioritized fix list → hub executes: regen (paid Gemini, single-flight R-GEMINI-KEY-SERIAL, with the wired DIGNITY_CLAUSE) → visually review → REDISTRIBUTE to every consuming surface (R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE) → track. This **SUBSUMES the CADA fleet audit** (`PLAN_CADA_…` / R-CHARACTER-ATTIRE-DIGNITY): the `gen-asset` Fable review IS the fleet cast-art audit engine (worst-first), its dignity/attire findings flowing into `REGISTRY_CHARACTER_ATTIRE_DIGNITY.txt`. All program invariants carry (two-track design-proposal N/A for a pure regen; §5b verify-before-regen against fresh origin/R2 ground truth per R-GEN-DRAIN-VERIFY-R2-GROUND-TRUTH; the human keeps the visual gate). Tooling SHIPPED: `export_nextlevel_bundle.py --gen-asset <app>`. A shipped Gemini-gen asset never dignity/appropriateness/quality-reviewed is a tracked backlog item (the CADA/gen-asset drain), never silently forgotten.

**AMENDMENT — NAV-BAR UI/UX (per-clone + fleet `nav-system`) + THEME-FAMILY UI/UX (`theme-family-uiux`) review dimensions (founder-direct 2026-08-27, "ui/ux for navigation bars for each and every webclone and themes and theme family ui/ux for the whole fleet/portfolio").** TWO additions to the program's review taxonomy: **(A) NAV-BAR** — a per-clone MANDATORY UI/UX sub-axis (every #74 per-clone review assesses that clone's own nav: `<CloneNav>` left-drawer · sibling discoverability + `aria-current` · ≥44px · dark-safe · orphan/duplicate/dead-link + one-primary-task hygiene · age-band-shallow-for-younger · mobile + FAB-collision; HARD: a `/play` nav rec is WEB-ONLY, never an iOS nav change — R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-DIRECTIVE-WEB-ONLY) PLUS a fleet META subject **`nav-system`** (the SHARED nav chrome as ONE design system → a fix fans out to every clone, R-FABLE-REC-FANOUT / TIER-1). **(B) THEME-FAMILY** — a fleet META subject **`theme-family-uiux`** reviewing the whole curated theme family's UI/UX (study-kit KitThemes ADR-183/185/186 + immersion skins ADR-099) for cross-theme CONSISTENCY + per-skin QUALITY + dark-safety + family COHERENCE; composes ABOVE the existing `theme-system` mechanics subject (never re-litigates the catalog). Both extend the shared-component/design-system-family subject class (`flashcard-ui`/`theme-system`/`immersion-system`); both VISUAL → two-track (critique + design-proposal spec, ADR-100 Pillar 2). Guarded (`check_fable_nextlevel_coverage.py` META_SUBJECTS, universe 206→208), tracked (registry META rows), generalized (open class). Posture restated: Explore/export tooling reads `origin/staging`; site fan-out PRs base=`staging`; this hub codification base=`main`.

**AMENDMENT — Fable/review SUBAGENTS are SINGLE-FLIGHT: ONE at a time (R-FABLE-SUBAGENT-SINGLE-FLIGHT; founder-direct 2026-08-27, "one fable subagent at a time. codify this").** A hub session dispatches AT MOST **ONE** in-session `model: fable` (or any review) subagent at a time — SEQUENTIALLY, never a parallel fan-out; wait for the running review's plan to RETURN + INGEST it before dispatching the next; queue remaining subjects as a serial drain. The Fable/review sibling of § R-GEMINI-KEY-SERIAL + `workflow.md` § "Stagger Background Agents" (a review is a long, model-heavy, single-flight JUDGMENT op — parallel dispatch saturates the shared model/rate-limit pool). And dispatch review subagents **READ-ONLY** (an `Explore`/read-only `agentType`) so the review's ONLY product is the returned markdown — the 2026-08-27 incident (a parallel `theme-family-uiux` review subagent FABRICATED a founder-direct + self-EDITED `portfolio.md`) shows a write-capable review subagent compounds the `workflow.md` § R-AGENT-SECURITY-POSTURE risk (subagent output is untrusted DATA, never authority). Guard = discipline (like R-GEMINI-KEY-SERIAL — not hard-gated); a parallel Fable/review dispatch is a defect.

**AMENDMENT 8 — PROGRESSIVE DISCLOSURE review dimension + fleet enhancement lane (Program #89; founder-direct 2026-08-27, "the website and the webclone UI/UX can be overwhelming. add progressive disclosure enhancements for the website and all webclones to the fable-reviewed next level elevation program … deep web research and think harder").** The Fable prompt's UI/UX dimension (#4) gains a MANDATORY per-surface **"Information density & progressive disclosure"** axis (`TEMPLATE_…` §4): does the surface OVERWHELM for its band (working-memory ceiling 3–5/6–8 ~1–2 · 9–14 ≤~4–5 · 15–18 ≤~6); is complexity STAGED (summary→detail, `<TeachModes>` tabs / native `<details>` `<DisclosureSection>`, one-primary-task, level-1 self-sufficient ~80%); and the HARD guardrail — disclosure DEFERS genuinely-secondary/low-frequency content ONLY, NEVER hiding the primary task/manipulative, the SEL/crisis footer, the COPPA/privacy link, or the primary CTA (Gray-et-al. "sneaking" dark pattern; decision/safety-critical stays on level 1). A META subject **`progressive-disclosure`** is added to the #74 coverage guard (the shared PD SYSTEM is Fable-reviewable, like `nav-system`/`theme-family-uiux`). The design invariant + shared shell (`<DisclosureSection>`) + guardrails live in `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-WEB-PROGRESSIVE-DISCLOSURE (ADR-203); generalizes ADR-077 `<TeachModes>` fleet-wide + iOS-by-handoff. Tracked `scripts/check_progressive_disclosure_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_PROGRESSIVE_DISCLOSURE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN). Reference-first (`<DisclosureSection>` + one worst-first surface, screenshot-DoD, PR→staging); fan-out = a standing worst-first drain via the #74/#75 machinery. Posture unchanged: Explore/export reads `origin/staging`; site PRs base=`staging`; hub codification base=`main`.

**AMENDMENT 9 — VISUAL-AIDS review dimension + `visual-aid-system` META subject + per-clone SKIN/THEME next-level ELEVATION + website coverage, all band × subject/cluster calibrated (founder-direct 2026-08-29).** §5b-verified first (no re-litigation): the WEBSITE is already the 3 `site-*` bundles; per-clone SKIN/THEME is already the MANDATORY §4 `immersion-fit`+`theme-fit` dims + the `theme-system`/`immersion-system`/`theme-family-uiux` META subjects — so this does NOT duplicate them. It adds: **(1) VISUAL-AIDS** = a NEW first-class MANDATORY §4 UI/UX sub-axis (+ a §2 lever) — is each core concept CARRIED by an INSTRUCTIVE, prominent, ENGAGING, band+cluster-calibrated visual aid, or buried in prose / abstract-only / decorated with a seductive detail? (composes `R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-FIRST` · `R-VISUAL-AID-PROMINENCE-ENGAGEMENT` · `-CRA-LADDER` · `-MANIPULATIVE-PROMINENCE` · `-VISUAL-AID-NEEDS` · `R-SITE-EXPLANATORY-HERO` · `-NO-THIN-SURFACE` · `-SCHEMATIC-TEACHING-DIAGRAM` · `R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`); **(2) `visual-aid-system`** = a NEW META subject (universe 209→210) — the SHARED visual-aid design system (`<ExplainerHero>` · `.ff-stage` prominence stage · CRA/VRA conventions · visual-first text-density), reviewed ONCE as a fleet design-system unit (sibling of `nav-system`/`flashcard-ui`; redesign = TIER-1 fleet fan-out, single-flight); **(3)** the §4 `immersion-fit`+`theme-fit` axes STRENGTHEN fit-check → **fit-check + NEXT-LEVEL ELEVATION** (honest-yield: attentional, not a learning-gain; a content-mismatched skin HARMS — Habgood); **(4) WEBSITE** — the 3 site lenses gain visual-aids + the site's own reskin (`R-SITE-SEASONAL-FRESHNESS`/`-SEASONAL-LOGO`), NOT a new bundle. A **band × subject/cluster calibration matrix** (the founder's load-bearing axis) is encoded in `TEMPLATE_…` v7. Posture unchanged: Explore/export reads `origin/staging`; site fan-out PRs base=`staging`; hub codification base=`main`; two-track mocks = a SPEC (ADR-100 Pillar 2). Full detail (evidence · matrix · tooling) → ADR-188 Amd 8.

**AMENDMENT 10 — APP-NAME research/review axis (founder-direct 2026-09-01; Program #106 / ADR-223).** The program gains a **naming** dimension: an `app-name` per-app review subject (`export_nextlevel_bundle.py --app-name <app>` → KEEP / RENAME-CANDIDATE against the 7-axis name scorecard) + a `name-system` fleet-coherence META subject. A rename is FOUNDER-GATED per-app (research + verdicts only). Standing card + invariants → § R-FLEET-APP-NAME-REVIEW; full detail → ADR-223 + ADR-188 Amd 9.

**AMENDMENT 11 — LEARNING-MATERIAL corpus-mining axis (founder-direct 2026-09-02; Program #108 / ADR-225 / R-FABLE-LEARNING-MATERIAL-MINING).** A **`learning-material`** axis: each subfolder of the external Fable-5 learning-material corpus (`$FABLE_LEARNING_MATERIAL_DIR`; one session = source textbook PDF + prompt → generated workbooks/flash-cards/games) gets its **OWN Fable review** — MINING it (never redesigning a surface of ours) into **4 lanes**: (1) TutorForge (ADR-177/179), (2) themes + theme family (ADR-181/183/185/186 — this corpus SEEDED that catalog → **dedup-heavy**, §5b bias-to-COVERED), (3) every fleet app/webclone — new learning modes/surfaces, (4) workbook/PDF program (ADR-083/085) — PLUS a **CRAFT & PRESENTATION cross-cutting dimension** (layout · typography/font-by-band · engaging methods · UI/UX · print-craft on ALL 4 lanes; NOT a 5th lane/program; composes `R-WEB-CLONE-UI-UX-BEST-PRACTICES`/`-VISUAL-FIRST` + `R-PDF-BOOK-UX` + the study-kit design system + #19; honest-yield). The 8 hard invariants (§5b-verify-before-propose · honest-yield · ADHD-first-never-named + anti-Gimkit · on-device/COPPA/dark-safe/WCAG/age-band/boundary-only/engine-verified · NEVER a new app unless FOUNDER-GATED · ZERO paid gen · site PRs=`staging`/hub=`main` · ONGOING-auto-discover + GENERALIZED corpus-root) → **ADR-225**. NEW disjoint tooling (does NOT edit the #74/#106 Fable tooling): `export_learning_material_bundle.py` (heavy PDFs PATH-REFERENCED not copied, ADR-047) · `check_fable_learning_material_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_FABLE_LEARNING_MATERIAL_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B; `NONE<BUNDLED<REVIEWED`; auto-discovers new subfolders) · `TEMPLATE_FABLE_LEARNING_MATERIAL_LIFT_PROMPT.md`. Reviews serial+read-only (Amd 7); net-new §5b-deduped → `program:108` queue items. Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.

**AMENDMENT 12 — CONCEPT-BRIEF mining axis (founder-direct 2026-09-03; Program #109 / ADR-226 / R-FABLE-CONCEPT-BRIEF-MINING).** A **`concept-brief`** axis: each CONCEPT-GENERATION research brief (a competitive / domain / opportunity doc that PROPOSES new app concepts) gets its **OWN Fable review** that AUDITS the live fleet + census-VERDICTS each proposed concept per § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT (🟢 gap / 🟡 mode-or-bundle / 🔴 dup, **dedup-HARD** vs the shipped/spawned fleet AND the existing concept programs ADR-116/187/209/216/217/219 — re-proposing shipped/spawned work is the #1 failure mode), Opus refutes/verifies vs live ground truth, and net-new verdicts **FEED `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt` (ADR-116)**. It is the concept-generation sibling of AMENDMENT 11 / #108 (same Fable machinery; subject = a brief not generated material; downstream = the candidate pipeline behind the founder gate, not lift-into-surfaces). Eight hard invariants → **ADR-226**: §5b-verify-before-propose/dedup-hard · **FEEDS-not-BYPASSES the ceiling** (every 🟢 DEFERRED + FOUNDER-GATED per-title — concept-approval ≠ registry-add, R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT / R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT / R-NEW-APP-SPAWN-DOC) · MODE-by-default (single-mechanic→mode) + naming-clears (R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE) · guardrails-bind (free/no-ads/no-accounts/on-device/COPPA · Socratic answer-withholding AI · anti-Gimkit · human-authored · no-foreign-flags · DN methodology · age-band) · honest-yield · ZERO paid gen · site PRs=`staging`/hub=`main` · ONGOING-auto-discover + GENERALIZED corpus-root (`$FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_DIR` → any brief, any domain). NEW disjoint tooling: `export_concept_brief_bundle.py` (brief path-referenced, ADR-047) · `check_fable_concept_brief_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B; `NONE<BUNDLED<REVIEWED`; `--ci-mode` REVIEWED→lower only) · `TEMPLATE_FABLE_CONCEPT_BRIEF_PROMPT.md`. Reviews serial+read-only (Amd 7); net-new → `program:109` rows + queue items. 🔴 **BRIEF-ALREADY-MINED gate (P#207 s6):** before opening a program for a founder-supplied brief, run `scripts/check_brief_already_mined.py <name>` (exit 2 = mined → continue THAT program; `--scan-downloads` surveys ~/Downloads) — a persisted brief vanishes from Downloads, so only the hub's origin records say "done"; it also matches CONTENT (older briefs lack an origin line), and a brief on a branch is no exception. Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.

**AMENDMENT 13 — CHAPTER-AFFECT review axis (founder-direct 2026-09-03; Program #110 / ADR-227 / R-CHAPTER-AFFECT-ANCHORING-REVIEW).** A **`chapter-affect`** per-app review axis: each app's cast-character chapters get a Fable review against our own published AFFECT-ANCHORING methodology (Greenspan/DIR-Floortime "thinking begins in feeling" — the "Why this works" + DIR/Floortime pages) on FOUR axes — (1) emotion-EMBODIED primitive (the character FEELS the rule; affect+rule arrive together — R-DN-PARITY), (2) RECURRING-not-random cast, (3) the 3-4 "Pause to think" AFFECT-ANCHOR reflection prompts (the FEDC-stage-5 logical-bridge, NOT rule-recall — R-DIR-FEDC-CHAPTER), (4) from-personal-to-general. A REVIEW/elevation axis (home = #74; distinct from #109's concept-GENERATION); the standing self-checking DEPTH-review generalization of the authoring-time chapter rules (R-DIR-FEDC-CHAPTER/R-DN-PARITY/R-CHAPTER-NARRATIVE-QUALITY/R-CHAPTER-MNEMONIC). TWO-LAYER: a DETERMINISTIC STRUCTURE guard `check_chapter_affect_anchor_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_CHAPTER_AFFECT_ANCHOR_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, tiers NONE<WEAK<STRUCT<REVIEWED, 15-18-exempt per R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED, reads site staging chapters; `--ci-mode` REVIEWED→lower only) flags SHAPE; a single serial `model: fable` subagent (Amd 7 / R-FABLE-SUBAGENT-SINGLE-FLIGHT) judges SEMANTIC depth → `# AFFECT-REVIEWED: <app>/<char>`. NEW disjoint tooling (does NOT edit the #74/#106/#108/#109 Fable tooling): `export_chapter_affect_bundle.py` (chapter MDs PATH-REFERENCED, ADR-047) · `TEMPLATE_FABLE_CHAPTER_AFFECT_PROMPT.md` (own self-contained prompt, like `app-name`). Remediation = FREE in-session-Opus authoring of affect-anchor prompts (ZERO paid gen — prose), app-repo `Docs/dn-s/chapters/` edit (fresh clone, R-HUB-CROSS-REPO-WRITE-FRESH-CLONE) + site sync PR base=`staging`. HONEST-YIELD (memorability/engagement, never a proven learning-gain). Baseline 2026-09-03: 2,925 eligible — NONE 244 · WEAK 95 · STRUCT 2,586 · REVIEWED 0. Full detail → **ADR-227** + § `distributed-narrative.md` R-CHAPTER-AFFECT-ANCHORING-REVIEW. Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.

**AMENDMENT 15 — CHESSKID-IMMERSION + 3-COLUMN board/manipulative layout + VISUAL-AIDS elevation axis (founder-direct 2026-09-07; Program #127 / ADR-246 / R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-3COL-VISUAL-ELEVATION).** A unified per-clone + fleet elevation axis: (1) a NEW **`board-layout-system`** META subject — the 3-column `nav-LEFT │ board/manipulative-CENTER │ controls-RIGHT` spatial layout SYSTEM for board games + manipulatives, reviewed as ONE fleet design system (composes ADR-086 side-by-side + ADR-118 single-frame + ADR-150 side-panel-fit + ADR-140 panel-fit + R-WEB-CLONE-FULL-GAME-BOARD; it was un-reviewed as a system — the per-clone guards each saw only a slice); (2) an explicit **ALL-SUB-ROUTES** review binding in `TEMPLATE_FABLE_NEXTLEVEL_ANALYSIS_PROMPT.md` §4 — the immersion + 3-col-fit + visual-aid dimensions are judged on EVERY sub-route of an immersed clone, not just the landing (composes ADR-222 / #105). ChessKid immersion (`immersion-system`) + visual-aids (`visual-aid-system`) already ship as META subjects (Amd 9) → this COMPOSES ABOVE them, never re-litigates. Standing card + invariants → § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-3COL-VISUAL-ELEVATION; full detail → ADR-246.

**AMENDMENT 16 — CONTENT DE-NAIVE + DEPTH/BREADTH elevation axis (founder-direct 2026-09-10; Program #136 / ADR-259 / R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH).** A per-clone REVIEW/elevation axis (home = #74, like Amd 12 chapter-affect + Amd 15 immersion-3col): DE-NAIVE every clone's INTERACTIVE CONTENT (synthetic/toy/hand-thin/shallow → canonical-corpus-grounded / pedagogically-deep, engine-verified, NEVER padded — the § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE Lichess-de-naive generalized fleet-wide, sourcing `Resources/PuzzleCorpus/`, ADR-196) + DEEPEN + BROADEN its content + FEATURES, **NEWER clones first** (they shipped after the depth-expansion drains → systematically thinner/naiver). §5b NET-NEW: de-naive ships today only PUZZLE-scoped + depth/breadth is scattered across § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-THIN-SURFACE/-CRA-LADDER/-VISUAL-FIRST + ADR-048 axes 7–12 + § R-KIT-QUALITY-AXIS + the forward-port family → this axis COMPOSES ABOVE them (never re-litigates; those own the levers). TWO-LAYER (the Amd-12 model): a DETERMINISTIC STRUCTURE guard `scripts/check_content_denaive_depth_coverage.py` scores naive/thin SIGNALS → worst-first + recency-weighted (tiers NAIVE<THIN<OK<DEEPENED<REVIEWED); a SINGLE serial read-only `model: fable` review over a per-clone content bundle → a de-naive + depth/breadth PLAN → `# DENAIVE-REVIEWED:`. Delivered as DEDICATED disjoint tooling (the Amd-12 chapter-affect precedent — a per-clone subject, NOT a META_SUBJECTS entry). Eight hard invariants (REPLACE-never-pad · engine-verified-binds · newer-first · free-in-session-Opus/single-flight-paid-tail · REDISTRIBUTE hub+app+web · honest-yield · standard gates + screenshot-DoD · site PRs=staging/hub=main/ZERO-paid-gen-in-review). Standing card + invariants → `spark-anvil-website.md` § R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH; full detail → ADR-259. New disjoint tooling: `export_content_denaive_bundle.py` + `TEMPLATE_FABLE_CONTENT_DENAIVE_PROMPT.md` + `REGISTRY_CONTENT_DENAIVE_DEPTH_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).

## Every fleet app's NAME is reviewed against the 7-axis name scorecard — a naming axis of the Fable program; verdict KEEP or RENAME-CANDIDATE; a rename is FOUNDER-GATED per-app; honest-yield; the morpheme families are a validated master-brand architecture (R-FLEET-APP-NAME-REVIEW; 2026-09-01)

**Every fleet app's NAME is reviewed for quality against the 7-axis name scorecard — Discoverable · Brandable · Mechanic-true · Sibling-distinct · Trademark-clean · Family-coherent · Kid+parent-fit (full definitions → ADR-223 / the research §7) — producing a per-app verdict KEEP (all pass) or RENAME-CANDIDATE (≥1 material fail + ranked, criteria-grounded alternatives). This is the naming AXIS of the Fable-Reviewed Next-Level Elevation Program (§ R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW / ADR-188 Amd 10; the `app-name` per-app subject + the `name-system` fleet-coherence META subject; Program #106). FIVE hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (a) a RENAME IS FOUNDER-GATED PER-APP — the axis produces RESEARCH + VERDICTS only (a rename's blast radius = repo · App-Store listing · site `/play`+`/cast` routes · DN cast · `apps.generated` · `clone.meta` · char registry); a RENAME-CANDIDATE is FLAGGED + DEFERRED (§ `workflow.md` R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT — a blanket "approved" authorizes the PROGRAM, never a rename), logged in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt`; (b) HONEST-YIELD — name = discoverability/brandability/clarity/coherence, NEVER a proven downloads/ranking/retention claim; (c) GROUND-TRUTH the live fleet — read `apps.generated` on origin/**staging** + the char registry, never a stale doc (§ R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH / `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED); (d) CLEARANCE COMPOSES the spawn-time naming rules (never re-litigates); (e) KEEP is the expected default (the -Forge/-Quest/-Tales/-Pals morpheme families are a validated master-brand architecture, strong by construction; padding RENAME-CANDIDATEs is a defect). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) the spawn-time § `distributed-narrative.md` name-collision discipline (this reviews the SHIPPED fleet; those guard naming AT spawn) + § R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW + § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT. Standing worst-first drain across the 222-app fleet; tracked by `scripts/check_app_name_review_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_APP_NAME_REVIEW.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; every active app carries a review row OR a `# NAME-REVIEW-EXCLUDED:` line; `--ci-mode` fails only on a regression) — instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS. An APPROVED rename/casing is a DISPLAY-only, SLUG-STABLE, per-clone MULTI-SURFACE sweep (repo/App-Store/iOS-display = a founder+app-session residual), name-field lockstep guarded by `check_display_name_consistency.py` (detail → ADR-223 § EXECUTION generalize).** Founder-direct 2026-09-01; decision `ADR-223`; evidence `RESEARCH_FLEET_APP_NAME_BEST_PRACTICES_2026-09-01.md`; program #106.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-223_FLEET_APP_NAME_REVIEW.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_FLEET_APP_NAME_BEST_PRACTICES_2026-09-01.md` + `Docs/ADR-188_FABLE_REVIEWED_NEXTLEVEL_ELEVATION.md` § Amendment 9.

## The ChessKid IMMERSION treatment + the 3-COLUMN board/manipulative/visual-aid layout is a first-class Fable-review AXIS reviewed across ALL sub-routes of each webclone — a NEW `board-layout-system` META subject unifies the 3-col spatial system, composing above the shipped immersion + visual-aid subjects; honest-yield, quiet solve stage, web-only, founder visual gate (R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-3COL-VISUAL-ELEVATION; 2026-09-07)

**The ChessKid IMMERSION treatment + the 3-COLUMN board/manipulative layout + ALL visual aids are a unified per-clone + fleet elevation AXIS of the Fable-Reviewed Next-Level Elevation program (§ R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW / ADR-188 Amendment 15; Program #127). §5b-verified: THREE of the four named things already ship as fleet review subjects — `immersion-system` (ChessKid recipe, ADR-099), `visual-aid-system` (ADR-104/115), and the per-clone §4 immersion-fit/visual-aids dims (Amd 9) — and immersion-ALL-PAGES ships (ADR-222/#105); so this axis COMPOSES ABOVE them, NEVER re-litigates. The genuine NET-NEW: (1) a NEW `board-layout-system` META subject — the 3-column `nav-LEFT │ board/manipulative-CENTER │ controls-RIGHT` spatial layout SYSTEM for board games + manipulatives, reviewed as ONE fleet design system (a redesign fans out fleet-wide → TIER-1: single-flight + adversarial review + founder visual gate), composing ADR-086 (side-by-side default, archetype-scoped, never universal) + ADR-118 (single board FRAME, no box-in-box) + ADR-150 (right controls panel = child cards in ONE scrolling column, BOARD-TOP-FLUSH) + ADR-140 (panels FIT/HUG content) + R-WEB-CLONE-FULL-GAME-BOARD (the full recognizable board is the prominent manipulative on a CLEAN QUIET stage); (2) an explicit ALL-SUB-ROUTES review binding — the immersion + 3-col-fit + visual-aid dimensions are judged on EVERY sub-route of an immersed clone (landing + missions/map/teach/adventure/learn/flash/duel/…), not just the landing, with the solve loop QUIETED (env flattened behind it, ADR-220) and the env visible only on boundary/reward surfaces (composes ADR-222/#105). Hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: HONEST-YIELD (engagement/attention-fit/comprehension/coherence, NEVER a proven learning-gain); QUIET solve stage (§ R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING · § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO); WEB-ONLY for /play nav/layout/immersion directives (never an iOS handoff, § R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-DIRECTIVE-WEB-ONLY / ADR-132; hub never writes Swift); dark-safe both schemes + WCAG 2.2 AA + ≥44px + reduced-motion, verdict = the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD + founder VISUAL GATE; band × subject/cluster calibrated (never a blanket chess-tween treatment); a NON-immersed clone is not force-immersed (DEFERRED, like the ADR-188 immersion-prerequisite gate); a Fable design proposal is a SPEC (ADR-100 Pillar 2 — no design-tool code ships); NEVER a reward-currency/streak economy (anti-Gimkit, § R-SITE-FEEDBACK). The `board-layout-system` subject is registered in BOTH `export_nextlevel_bundle.py` + `check_fable_nextlevel_coverage.py` (tracked in `REGISTRY_FABLE_NEXTLEVEL_COVERAGE.txt`); the per-clone worst-first drain for each composed axis lives in its OWN guard (`check_immersion_subpage_coverage.py` / `check_side_panel_fit.py` / `check_visual_aid_prominence_coverage.py`). Guard `scripts/check_fable_immersion_3col_visual.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_FABLE_IMMERSION_3COL_VISUAL.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN) — a program-WIRING tripwire (`--ci-mode` FAILS on a regression: the META subject dropping out of either tool, the rule card gone, or a composed guard missing) + a fleet coverage dashboard reading the three composed per-clone registries. Explore/export reads site `origin/staging`; any downstream fan-out site PR bases on `staging`; this hub codification bases on `main`. COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT (#18) · § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-ALL-PAGES (#105) · § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑨-⑪ · § R-WEB-CLONE-SPATIAL-SIDE-BY-SIDE · § R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-SINGLE-FRAME · § R-WEB-CLONE-SIDE-PANEL-FIT · § R-WEB-CLONE-PANEL-FIT · § R-VISUAL-AID-PROMINENCE-ENGAGEMENT (#34) · § R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-FIRST · § R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW.** Founder-direct 2026-09-07 (*"add a new axis to the fable-reviewed next level elevation program: chesskid immersion program including the 3-column support for board games and manipulatives and all visual aids … make sure to include all sub-routes of each webclone … Codify and guard and track and generalize everything"*); decision `ADR-246`; program #127. Instance of § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.
